## 2\. CLI-First Application Implementation Ideas

A CLI tool (e.g., `loto-pro`) would allow power users to run analyses without the overhead of a GUI.

### Core Features

- **`loto-pro analyze --limit 10`**: Fetches the last 10 results and identifies "brigaded" or "married" pairs automatically.
- **`loto-pro generate --exclude 8,10,15,24 --fixed 7,11`**: Generates 15-number combinations based on the user's specific constraints.
- **`loto-pro sync`**: Updates a local SQLite database with the latest official results from the Caixa repository.

## 3\. Implementation of the Image Analysis into the CLI

To replicate the visual grid from your screenshot in a terminal environment, we apply a structured engineering approach.

### **Engineering Design: The Grid Renderer**

**1\. Problem Statement**

- **Expected:** A visual representation of the last 10 draws (Numbers 01-25) where drawn numbers are highlighted green and missed numbers remain white/gray.
- **Actual:** Current terminal output is just raw text or lists, which makes pattern recognition (like cycles or streaks) difficult.

**2\. Hypotheses for Rendering**

- **H1 (ASCII Art):** Use `[X]` for green and `[ ]` for white. **Signal:** Low visual clarity but high compatibility.
- **H2 (ANSI Color Blocks):** Use `01`, `02` with ANSI background colors (`\u001b[42m`). **Signal:** High readability; matches the screenshot's color scheme.
- **H3 (Braille/Unicode Symbols):** Use dots to represent the grid. **Signal:** Too abstract for number identification.

**3\. Investigation Steps (Implementation Order)**

1. **Data Structure:** Model the draw as a boolean array `bool[25]`.
2. **Color Mapping:** Map `true` to `bg_green + fg_black` and `false` to `bg_white + fg_black`.
3. **Buffer Printing:** Iterate through 10 contests, printing the contest ID in purple (as seen in the screenshot) followed by the 25-number grid.

**4\. Root Cause for Poor CLI UX (and the Fix)**

- **Issue:** Standard terminal widths vary.
- **Fix:** Use a "Fixed-Width Column" strategy. Each number cell must be exactly 3 characters (e.g., `01`).
- **Minimal Blast Radius:** Implement a `Grid` class that handles its own padding so it doesn't break other text logs.

### **Implementation Snippet (Python Example)**

Python

```
from rich.console import Console
from rich.table import Table

def render_loto_grid(data):
    console = Console()
    table = Table(show_header=False, padding=(0,0), box=None)
    
    # Create columns for Contest ID + 25 numbers
    for _ in range(26): table.add_column()

    for row in data:
        # Row[0] = Contest ID (Purple)
        cells = [f"[bold white on purple] {row['id']} [/]"]
        for num in range(1, 26):
            if num in row['drawn']:
                cells.append(f"[black on green] {num:02d} [/]")
            else:
                cells.append(f"[black on white] {num:02d} [/]")
        table.add_row(*cells)
    
    console.print(table)
```

**5\. Validation & Monitoring**

- **Regression Test:** Pass a draw with number 01 and 25 to ensure edge-case rendering.
- **Monitoring:** Use a `terminal_width_check` to alert the user if their window is too narrow to display the 25-column grid without wrapping.

### 🔄 Phase 1: PRD Audit & Gap Detection

Analyzing the essence of **image\_6772b3.jpg** reveals the following missing edge cases and ambiguous constraints:

- **Correlation Matrix (The "Bridge" Gap):** The dashboard shows individual tendencies, but lotteries often have "married pairs" (numbers that frequently appear together). A gap exists in the **Data Contract** for cross-correlation metrics.
- **Atraso Invariant:** The "Atraso" (Delay) logic must have a strict data contract. If a number is drawn, the delay *must* reset to 0 in the next cycle.
- **Outlier Detection (Zebra Patterns):** The current model prioritizes "high frequency," but system invariants should also monitor for "improbable" draws (e.g., 15 even numbers) which occur in <1% of draws.

### 🔄 Phase 2: The Premortem

**Scenario:** The prediction engine fails to produce a winning ticket within 50 games.

- **Root Cause 1:** The system over-indexed on historical frequency (Saída), failing to account for the "regression to the mean" where cold numbers eventually fire.
- **Root Cause 2:** Technical debt in the `Sequence` parser led to incorrect "Streak" calculations during cycle transitions (e.g., from 3669 to 3670).

### 🔄 Phase 3: ADR (Architectural Decision Record)

**Decision:** Implement a Hybrid Generator.

- **Context:** Pure random generation is too broad; strict frequency filtering is too narrow.
- **Approach:**
	- **Rule-Based Filter:** Discard sets that violate established invariants (e.g., Primes < 3 or Sum > 220).
		- **Weighted Monte Carlo:** Select numbers from the "Green" and "Yellow" categories (from **image\_6772b3.jpg**) using weights derived from their current **Atraso** and **Frequency**.
- **Consequences:** Increased computational overhead but significantly reduced "junk" ticket generation.

### 🔄 Phase 4: Execution Blueprint (Definition of Done)

1. **Safe Refactoring:** Transition from the current PARA structure in Obsidian to the **Workspace-Relational Model** to house the statistical logic.
2. **Observability:** Implement **OpenTelemetry** to track the "prediction accuracy" metric—comparing suggested numbers to actual draws over 10 games.
3. **Data Layer:** Use **Postgres** with narrow transaction scopes to store contest history, ensuring indexed lookups for the `SEQUÊNCIA` and `FREQUÊNCIA` columns.

### 🔍 Tendency Analysis & Unique Dashboard Plan

Using the **Systematic Debugging** methodology, the dashboard aims to replace guesswork by identifying statistical anomalies (the "bugs" in random distribution).

#### 1\. Problem Statement

- **Expected:** Balanced distribution across columns.
- **Actual:** High "Atraso" (Delay) in numbers 20 and 25 (3 and 2 draws late) vs. high "Sequência" (Streak) in 23 (11 draws straight).

#### 2\. Hypotheses for Next Draw (3670)

| --- | --- | --- |
| **1** | **Regression to the Mean:** Overdue numbers (20, 25) will fire to correct high delay. | Signal: ATRASO > 2. |
| **2** | **Heat Preservation:** High-frequency numbers (06, 11, 07, 23, 10) will maintain their 75% streak. | Signal: FREQ% >= 75. |
| **3** | **Column 4 Correction:** Col 4 has the lowest overall output (SAIDA: 8, 9) and likely "corrects" upwards. | Signal: Sum of COL 4 drawn > 3. |

#### 3\. Investigation Steps (The Dashboard Engine)

1. **Stateful Tracking:** Implement a sliding window of the last 
	$$
	N
	$$
	 games to update **Atraso** and **Frequência** in real-time.
2. **Confirmation Signals:** The dashboard must flag numbers where `Atraso > (Historical Average Delay * 1.5)`.
3. **Blast Radius Assessment:** Evaluate "Excluded Numbers" (Red Flags) to ensure they don't break cycle-completion logic.

#### 4\. Unique Dashboard Features

- **Evidence-Driven Heatmap:** A grid that highlights numbers based on the "Evidence Chain" (Frequency + Atraso).
- **Regression Tester:** A module that runs the current selection against the last 10 draws to monitor how often the "Hot" vs "Cold" hypothesis would have won.
- **Safe Selection Engine:** A CLI tool that suggests combinations but includes a "Rollback Plan" warning if the selected patterns are too similar to a "Zebra" (improbable) draw.

**Architecture Decision:** For maximum performance and terminal compatibility, the data layer should store contest history as a bitmask in **Redis** or a structured **JSON**, allowing the CLI to render stats instantly without heavy database lookups.

## Technical Analysis: Collective vs. Independent Perimeter Variables

Since you are considering treating the **Moldura** (16 numbers) as a collective variable, you are moving toward **Spatial Frequency Analysis**. While Lotofácil is a random draw, observing how many numbers "land" in the perimeter versus the center can help identify distribution clusters that deviate from the mean.

### 1\. Perimeter (Moldura) vs. Center Logic

- **The Collective Approach**: You track the total count (e.g., 11 in this draw). This acts as a **macroscopic filter** to prune unlikely combinations (e.g., tickets with only 5 or 14 perimeter numbers, which statistically occur less frequently).
- **The Independent Approach**: You track the probability of each of the 16 numbers. This is a **microscopic filter** based on individual frequency.

I've been reviewing the provided materials, and it's clear you want to build a unique CLI dashboard to analyze lottery tendencies by comparing the last N games, using visual patterns like Fibonacci, Moldura, and Primes.

**Critique & Blind Spots:**
1. **The 'Everything Everywhere' Fallacy:** Showing *all* these patterns in a terminal will lead to cognitive overload and serious readability issues. A CLI demands conciseness. Cramming multiple statistical tables into one view will make it unreadable and hinder rapid decision-making.
*Alternative:* We need a composable UI, where specific modules are displayed on demand, or a unified 'Alerts' view that highlights statistically anomalous patterns (e.g., `Atraso > Média * 2`).

2. **Apophenia vs. Actionable Constraints:** Visualizing grids can mislead the brain into seeing predictive patterns where only random chance exists. These visual "tendencies" are more valuable as *hard constraint filters* to eliminate improbable combinations, not as direct predictors. The dashboard should emphasize actionable metrics like `Atraso` (Delay) and `Sai em Média` (Average Appearance) as filterable constraints.

To move forward and ensure we build a truly effective tool, I need to ask you some direct questions:

**Q1: Presentation Strategy – How should the CLI handle the complex statistical tables without causing visual overload?**
*   **Unified Risk Score:** Consolidate patterns into a single metric (e.g., "Zebra Index") with drill-down commands for focused analysis.
*   **Composable/Toggleable Views:** Show one or two tables at a time via arguments (e.g., `loto-pro dash --view primes`).
*   **Dense Multi-pane Dashboard:** Render a large, fixed layout with all tables using strict ANSI formatting.

**Q2: Primary Operational Goal – What is the main purpose of viewing these tendencies in the CLI?**
*   **Manual Hunch Validation:** I want to review stats to manually decide which numbers to pick or avoid.
*   **Hard Constraint Filtering:** I want to see anomalous patterns and instruct the engine to auto-filter combinations that violate them.
*   **Historical Auditing:** Purely for reviewing past draws and monitoring cycle completions, not for real-time bet generation.

**Q3: Data Handling – How will you manage the data source and state for computing `Atraso` and historical averages?**
*   **In-Memory / Local JSON:** Load a static file on run.
*   **Local DB (SQLite):** Maintain a synchronized database of all historical draws for fast querying.
*   **Live API Fetch:** Fetch the latest N draws on demand for always up-to-date data.**Clarifying Dashboard Requirements** I am currently focused on refining the core purpose and implementation strategy for the CLI dashboard. ```json
{
  "name": "write_file",
  "args": {
    "content": "# Lotofácil Tendency Dashboard & Evidence Engine\n\n## 1. Background & Motivation\nThe user seeks to integrate comprehensive statistical tendency dashboards (e.g., Odd/Even, Primes, Fibonacci, Frame/Center, Multiples of 3, Magic Numbers, and Repeated Numbers) into a CLI-first prediction application. Visualizing these patterns allows for structural analysis of lottery draws, but attempting to view all metrics simultaneously leads to visual noise, terminal wrapping, and cognitive overload (Apophenia). Therefore, the new feature must be robust, composable, and tied directly to systematic constraint filtering rather than purely manual \"hunch\" generation.\n\n## 2. Scope & Impact\n- **Impacted Modules**: \n  - `engine/cli/commands/dashboard.py` (Refactoring to support toggleable views/widgets).\n  - `engine/modules/patterns.py` (Adding missing `MÁGICOS`, `MÚLTIPLOS DE 3`, and `REPETIDAS` logic).\n  - `engine/modules/frequency.py` (Extending to calculate historical `Média` and current `Atraso` for structural patterns, not just single numbers).\n- **Data Architecture**: Leverages the existing `storage.py` module (`LOTTERY_STORAGE_POLICY='cache'`) which provides rapid loading of historical draws into Pandas DataFrames via DuckDB or Partitioned JSON.\n- **Constraints**: Must fit cleanly within standard terminal bounds (80-120 columns) and use `rich` for ANSI-colored rendering.\n\n## 3. Proposed Solution\nInstead of one dense \"Everything Everywhere\" dashboard, we will implement a **Composable Views & Evidence Engine**. \n\n### 3.1. The \"Evidence Engine\" Command (`loto-pro dash --view <pattern>`)\nWe will extend the CLI to support isolated visual tables for specific patterns. \n- **Available Views**: `impares_pares`, `primos`, `fibonacci`, `moldura`, `multiplos_3`, `magicos`, `repetidas`, and `alerts`.\n- **The \"Alerts First\" Default**: If no `--view` is specified, the dashboard defaults to an `alerts` view. This view scans all patterns and *only* displays rows where `Atraso >= (Média * 1.5)`, signifying a statistical anomaly (a pattern that is highly overdue).\n\n### 3.2. Table Structure (High-Fidelity ASCII/Rich)\nEach detailed view will render a `rich.Table` replicating the requested essence:\n```\n[ PATTERN NAME ]\nQTD | OCORRÊNCIA | MÉDIA (%) | SAI EM MÉDIA | ÚLT. VEZ SAIU | ATRASO | TREND\n```\n- **Conditional Formatting**: Rows where `Atraso` significantly exceeds `Sai em Média` will be highlighted in **Bold Red** to alert the user.\n- **Actionable Constraints**: The output will include a \"Suggested Filter\" snippet (e.g., `loto-pro suggest --force-fibonacci 5`) to bridge the gap between visual observation and systematic generation.\n\n### 3.3. Pattern Module Extensions\nUpdate `patterns.py` to calculate the missing pattern sets specifically for Lotofácil:\n- `MAGICOS = {5, 6, 7, 12, 13, 14, 19, 20, 21}`\n- `MULTIPLOS_3 = {n for n in range(1, 26) if n % 3 == 0}`\n- `REPETIDAS`: A function comparing `draw[N]` with `draw[N-1]`.\n\n## 4. Alternatives Considered\n- **Dense Multi-pane Dashboard**: Rejected to avoid terminal wrapping issues and cognitive overload.\n- **Live API Fetching per Render**: Rejected as it causes unacceptable latency. The existing DuckDB caching is retained for performance.\n- **Pure Manual Analysis**: Rejected as visualizing grids without statistical thresholds promotes Gambler's Fallacy. We enforce the `Atraso` vs `Média` calculation to ground the analysis in evidence.\n\n## 5. Implementation Steps\n\n### Phase 1: Pattern Logic Expansion\n1. Update `engine/modules/patterns.py` to include `get_magicos`, `get_multiplos_3`, and `get_repetidas`.\n2. Add a general `calculate_pattern_frequency(df, pattern_func)` in `engine/modules/frequency.py` that processes the loaded DataFrame to extract `Ocorrência`, `Média`, `Sai em Média` (1/Média), `Última Vez Que Saiu`, and `Atraso`.\n\n### Phase 2: Composable Dashboard UI\n1. # Lotofácil Tendency Dashboard & Evidence Engine

## 1. Background & Motivation
The user seeks to integrate comprehensive statistical tendency dashboards (e.g., Odd/Even, Primes, Fibonacci, Frame/Center, Multiples of 3, Magic Numbers, and Repeated Numbers) into a CLI-first prediction application. Visualizing these patterns allows for structural analysis of lottery draws, but attempting to view all metrics simultaneously leads to visual noise, terminal wrapping, and cognitive overload (Apophenia). Therefore, the new feature must be robust, composable, and tied directly to systematic constraint filtering rather than purely manual "hunch" generation.

## 2. Scope & Impact
- **Impacted Modules**: 
  - `engine/cli/commands/dashboard.py` (Refactoring to support toggleable views/widgets).
  - `engine/modules/patterns.py` (Adding missing `MÁGICOS`, `MÚLTIPLOS DE 3`, and `REPETIDAS` logic).
  - `engine/modules/frequency.py` (Extending to calculate historical `Média` and current `Atraso` for structural patterns, not just single numbers).
- **Data Architecture**: Leverages the existing `storage.py` module (`LOTTERY_STORAGE_POLICY='cache'`) which provides rapid loading of historical draws into Pandas DataFrames via DuckDB or Partitioned JSON.
- **Constraints**: Must fit cleanly within standard terminal bounds (80-120 columns) and use `rich` for ANSI-colored rendering.

## 3. Proposed Solution
Instead of one dense "Everything Everywhere" dashboard, we will implement a **Composable Views & Evidence Engine**. 

### 3.1. The "Evidence Engine" Command (`loto-pro dash --view <pattern>`)
We will extend the CLI to support isolated visual tables for specific patterns. 
- **Available Views**: `impares_pares`, `primos`, `fibonacci`, `moldura`, `multiplos_3`, `magicos`, `repetidas`, and `alerts`.
- **The "Alerts First" Default**: If no `--view` is specified, the dashboard defaults to an `alerts` view. This view scans all patterns and *only* displays rows where `Atraso >= (Média * 1.5)`, signifying a statistical anomaly (a pattern that is highly overdue).

### 3.2. Table Structure (High-Fidelity ASCII/Rich)
Each detailed view will render a `rich.Table` replicating the requested essence:
```
[ PATTERN NAME ]
QTD | OCORRÊNCIA | MÉDIA (%) | SAI EM MÉDIA | ÚLT. VEZ SAIU | ATRASO | TREND
```
- **Conditional Formatting**: Rows where `Atraso` significantly exceeds `Sai em Média` will be highlighted in **Bold Red** to alert the user.
- **Actionable Constraints**: The output will include a "Suggested Filter" snippet (e.g., `loto-pro suggest --force-fibonacci 5`) to bridge the gap between visual observation and systematic generation.

### 3.3. Pattern Module Extensions
Update `patterns.py` to calculate the missing pattern sets specifically for Lotofácil:
- `MAGICOS = {5, 6, 7, 12, 13, 14, 19, 20, 21}`
- `MULTIPLOS_3 = {n for n in range(1, 26) if n % 3 == 0}`
- `REPETIDAS`: A function comparing `draw[N]` with `draw[N-1]`.

## 4. Alternatives Considered
- **Dense Multi-pane Dashboard**: Rejected to avoid terminal wrapping issues and cognitive overload.
- **Live API Fetching per Render**: Rejected as it causes unacceptable latency. The existing DuckDB caching is retained for performance.
- **Pure Manual Analysis**: Rejected as visualizing grids without statistical thresholds promotes Gambler's Fallacy. We enforce the `Atraso` vs `Média` calculation to ground the analysis in evidence.

## 5. Implementation Steps

### Phase 1: Pattern Logic Expansion
1. Update `engine/modules/patterns.py` to include `get_magicos`, `get_multiplos_3`, and `get_repetidas`.
2. Add a general `calculate_pattern_frequency(df, pattern_func)` in `engine/modules/frequency.py` that processes the loaded DataFrame to extract `Ocorrência`, `Média`, `Sai em Média` (1/Média), `Última Vez Que Saiu`, and `Atraso`.

### Phase 2: Composable Dashboard UI
1. Modify `engine/cli/commands/dashboard.py` to accept a `--view` argument.
2. Build specific `rich.Table` builders for each pattern view.
3. Implement the `Alerts` view logic: iterate over all pattern functions, calculate stats, and filter for rows where `Atraso` is critically high.

### Phase 3: Systematic Integration
1. Ensure the `suggest` command (in `engine/cli/commands/suggest.py`) can accept arguments like `--filter-anomalies` which automatically hooks into the `Alerts` view logic to restrict generated tickets to correct the overdue patterns.

## 6. Verification
- **Unit Tests**: Write `pytest` cases in `tests/test_patterns.py` to ensure `Atraso` resets to 0 correctly when a pattern hits.
- **Regression Test**: Run `lottery dashboard lotofacil --view fibonacci --limit 3000` to verify performance (must execute under 500ms using the DuckDB backend).
- **Visual Check**: Resize terminal to 80 columns and ensure tables do not word-wrap destructively.

## 7. Migration & Rollback
- No database migrations are required as all calculations run dynamically off the existing `numbers` integer arrays.
- The existing comprehensive dashboard is moved to `--view all` or `--view legacy` to prevent breaking existing workflows of users accustomed to the monolithic view.

The feature must be robust, composable, and tied directly to systematic constraint filtering, while also providing an \"all-in-one\" intelligent overview for comprehensive analysis. Furthermore, the underlying statistical logic must be strictly decoupled from the CLI presentation layer to allow for future reuse in APIs or other UIs.\n\n## 2. Scope & Impact\n- **Impacted Modules**: \n  - `engine/cli/commands/analyze.py` & `engine/cli/commands/dashboard.py` (Adding support for composable views and an intelligent comprehensive layout).\n  - `engine/modules/patterns.py` (Adding missing logic for `MAGIC`, `MULTIPLES OF 3`, and `REPEATED`).\n  - `engine/modules/frequency.py` (Extending to calculate historical `Average` and current `Delay` for structural patterns).\n- **Data Architecture**: Leverages the existing `storage.py` module (`LOTTERY_STORAGE_POLICY='cache'`).\n- **Decoupling Constraint**: All statistical calculations, trend analysis, and cycle intelligence MUST return pure Python data structures (e.g., dicts, Pydantic models, or Pandas DataFrames) and MUST NOT contain any `rich` UI elements or terminal-specific formatting.\n\n## 3. Proposed Solution\nWe will implement a **Composable Views & Evidence Engine** that integrates seamlessly with the existing `analyze` and `dashboard` commands, built on top of a reusable data layer.\n\n### 3.1. Reusable Data Layer (Core Logic)\nCreate a new function/class in `engine/modules/frequency.py` or a dedicated `tendency.py` that processes the historical draws and returns a structured schema containing:\n- Pattern stats: QTY, Occurrence, Average (%), Avg Frequency, Last Seen, Delay, and Trend (Cycle/Row intelligence).\n- Cycle Intelligence: Detects momentum shifts across recent rows/draws (e.g., identifying if a pattern's delay is accelerating or reverting to the mean).\n\n### 3.2. The \"Evidence Engine\" Command Architecture\nWe will extend the CLI to support a `--view` option and layout preferences.\n- **CLI Usage**: `python -m engine.cli.main analyze <lottery> --module dashboard --view <pattern>`\n- **Available Views**: \n  - `odd_even`, `primes`, `fibonacci`, `frame`, `multiples_3`, `magic`, `repeated`.\n  - `alerts` (default): Only displays rows where `Delay >= (Average * 1.5)`.\n  - `complete` (or `all`): An intelligent, multi-section visual layout that displays all patterns. This view will support scrolling or a masonry-style grid (using `rich.Layout` or `rich.Columns`) to accommodate terminals of varying sizes, allowing the user to evaluate horizontal/vertical density.\n\n### 3.3. Table Structure & UI\nFor individual views, render a `rich.Table`:\n```\n[ PATTERN NAME ]\nQTY | OCCURRENCE | AVERAGE (%) | AVG FREQ | LAST SEEN | DELAY | CYCLE TREND\n```\n- **Conditional Formatting**: Rows where `Delay` significantly exceeds `Avg Frequency` will be highlighted in **Bold Red**.\n- **Cycle Intelligence**: The `CYCLE TREND` column will show a sparkline or momentum indicator based on the last N draws.\n- **Actionable Constraints**: Suggest filters (e.g., `lottery suggest lotofacil --force-fibonacci 5`).\n\n### 3.4. Pattern Module Extensions\nUpdate `patterns.py`:\n- `MAGIC = {5, 6, 7, 12, 13, 14, 19, 20, 21}`\n- `MULTIPLES_3 = {n for n in range(1, 26) if n % 3 == 0}`\n- `REPEATED`: A function comparing `draw[N]` with `draw[N-1]`.\n\n## 4. Alternatives Considered\n- **Tightly Coupled UI Logic**: Rejected. Mixing `rich` formatting into the calculation logic prevents reuse in REST APIs or web dashboards.\n- **Strictly Single-View Dashboard**: Rejected. The user explicitly requested an intelligent \"complete\" view to evaluate data density, so a flexible multi-pane layout must be provided as an option.\n\n## 5. Implementation Steps\n\n### Phase 1: Core Logic & Decoupling\n1. Update `engine/modules/patterns.py` with `MAGIC`, `MULTIPLES_3`, and `REPEATED`.\n2. Build a core data engine (e.g., `calculate_pattern_tendencies(df)`) that returns raw dictionaries containing `Occurrence`, `Average`, `Avg Freq`, `Last Seen`, `Delay`, and `Cycle Momentum`.\n   - *Note:* Some patterns could be saved/persisted into the storage layer (JSON, DuckDB), because some patterns are very linked to the draw/row.\n\n### Phase 2: Composable & Complete Dashboard UI\n1. Modify `engine/cli/commands/analyze.py` and `dashboard.py` to accept `--view` and optional layout flags.\n2. Build `rich` renderers that consume the pure data dicts.\n3. Implement the `complete` view using `rich.Layout` or `rich.Columns` to stack multiple pattern tables intelligently, providing a comprehensive \"Everything Everywhere\" experience that wraps/scrolls as needed. Make sure to provide different types of layout and composition for horizontal and vertical exploration of the data dicts.\n\n### Phase 3: Systematic Integration\n1. Update `suggest` command to accept `--filter-anomalies` which consumes the decoupled data engine to restrict generated tickets based on overdue patterns.\n\n## 6. Verification\n- **Unit Tests**: Test the decoupled data functions independently of the CLI to ensure API readiness.\n- **Visual Check**: Run `--view complete` on small and large terminal windows to evaluate the intelligent layout wrapping and cycle trend visualisations.\n\n## 7. Migration & Rollback\n- No database migrations required.\n- The new `complete` view serves as an upgrade to the existing monolithic dashboard, while `--view legacy` can be retained if necessary.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Dashboard Engine Requirements & Features\n1. **Stateful Tracking:** Implement a sliding window of the last `N` games to update **Atraso** (Delay) and **Frequência** (Frequency) in real-time.\n2. **Confirmation Signals:** Flag numbers where `Delay > (Historical Average Delay * 1.5)`.\n3. **Trend Heatmap**: Use `FREQ %` and `SEQ` to color-code numbers (Green for streaks, Red for high delays).\n4. **Cycle Monitor**: Alert the user when a number hasn't appeared in `X` draws.\n5. **Blast Radius Filter**: Filter generated combinations based on \"Common Traps\".\n6. **Hypothesis Tester**: Allow the user to input a hunch and calculate the `DELAY` and `FREQUENCY` impact of that choice.\n7. **Regression Tester**: A module that runs the current selection against the last 10 draws to monitor how often the \"Hot\" vs \"Cold\" hypothesis would have won.\n8. **Intersection View**: Show how many numbers currently satisfy both Magic and Prime patterns.\n\n---\n\n## 9. ASCII Mockups & UI Inspiration\n\n### Vertical Draw Analysis Dashboard\n```\n=====================================================================================================\n NUM | 60 61 62 63 64 65 66 67 68 69 | ATRASO ATUAL | SEQ. MÁX | FREQ % |        TENDENCY       \n-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n 01  | XX .. .. XX XX XX XX XX XX .. |      01      |    06    |  60%   | Delay Correction\n 02  | XX XX .. .. XX XX .. .. XX XX |      00      |    05    |  60%   | Stable Output\n ...\n=====================================================================================================\n```\n\n### Complete View Example\n```\n================================================================================\n [ PRO-LOTO CLI ] v3.0  |  CONCURSO: 3670 (PREV)  |  CICLO: 778 (D1)\n================================================================================\n\n--- [ SECTION 1: LAST RESULT (3669) ] ------------------------------------------\n DRAW: 02 03 04 05 08 09 10 11 12 15 16 17 22 23 24\n SUM: 181 | PR: 06 | FB: 04 | M3: 05 | MD: 11 | CT: 04 | RP: 10\n\n--- [ SECTION 3: PATTERN EVIDENCE STACK ] --------------------------------------\n MEASURE      | QTD | OCORR. | ATRASO | AVG | STATUS / ALERT\n -------------|-----|--------|--------|-----|-------------------------------\n FIBONACCI    |  5  |  1021  |    4   |  4  | CRITICAL ALERT <--- !!!\n REPETIDAS    |  9  |  1199  |    7   |  3  | EXTREME DELAY (2x AVG)\n MÁGICOS      |  5  |  1100  |    9   |  3  | ANOMALY (3x AVG)\n\n--- [ SECTION 4: THE EVIDENCE ENGINE (CATEGORIES) ] ----------------------------\n GREEN  (High Trend): 01 02 05 07 09 10 12 (High Saída/Freq)\n YELLOW (Neutral)   : 14 15 08 03 04 23 25 (Mid Stability)\n PINK   (Cold/Risky): 21 22 20 24 19 18 (Cycle Ends/Delays)\n\n--- [ SECTION 5: ACTIVE HYPOTHESES (DEBUGGING 3670) ] --------------------------\n H1: Pattern 'MÁGICOS 5' will fire to resolve Delay 9.\n H2: Pattern 'REPETIDAS 9' will fire to resolve Delay 7.\n================================================================================\n```",

1) // cols + 1\n        col = (n - 1) % cols + 1\n        \n        is_edge = (row == 1 or row == rows or col == 1 or col == cols)\n        if is_edge:\n            frame.append(n)\n        else:\n            center.append(n)\n            \n    return {\"frame\": frame, \"center\": center}\n\ndef get_line_column_distribution(numbers: List[int], cols: int = 10) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Count how many numbers fall into each row and column.\n    \"\"\"\n    row_counts = {}\n    col_counts = {}\n    \n    for n in numbers:\n        row = (n - 1) // cols + 1\n        col = (n - 1) % cols + 1\n        row_counts[row] = row_counts.get(row, 0) + 1\n        col_counts[col] = col_counts.get(col, 0) + 1\n        \n    return {\"rows\": row_counts, \"cols\": col_counts}\n\ndef get_pattern_string(numbers: List[int], cols: int = 10, max_n: int = 60) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Returns a string like '3-3-3-3-3' representing the row distribution.\"\"\"\n    lc = get_line_column_distribution(numbers, cols)\n    rows = math.ceil(max_n / cols)\n    return \"-\".join(str(lc[\"rows\"].get(r, 0)) for r in range(1, rows + 1))\n\ndef analyze_voids(draws: List[List[int]], cols: int = 10, max_n: int = 60, window: int = 5) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Identify sectors (rows, columns, quadrants) that have been empty for a number of draws.\n    \"\"\"\n    recent_draws = draws[-window:] if len(draws) >= window else draws\n    rows = math.ceil(max_n / cols)\n    \n    row_last_seen = {r: 0 for r in range(1, rows + 1)}\n    col_last_seen = {c: 0 for c in range(1, cols + 1)}\n    \n    # Track when each row/col was last 'hit'\n    for i, draw in enumerate(draws):\n        for n in draw:\n            r = (n - 1) // cols + 1\n            c = (n - 1) % cols + 1\n            row_last_seen[r] = i + 1\n            col_last_seen[c] = i + 1\n            \n    total_draws = len(draws)\n    row_delays = {r: total_draws - last for r, last in row_last_seen.items()}\n    col_delays = {c: total_draws - last for c, last in col_last_seen.items()}\n    \n    return {\n        \"row_delays\": row_delays,\n        \"col_delays\": col_delays,\n        \"cold_rows\": [r for r, d in row_delays.items() if d >= window],\n        \"cold_cols\": [c for c, d in col_delays.items() if d >= window]\n    }\n\ndef analyze_cycle(draws: List[List[int]], total_range: List[int]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Analyze the 'Cycle' of numbers.\n    A cycle is complete when all numbers in the total_range have appeared at least once.\n    \"\"\"\n    total_set = set(total_range)\n    seen = set()\n    cycles = []\n    current_cycle_start = 0\n    \n    for i, draw in enumerate(draws):\n        seen.update(draw)\n        if total_set.issubset(seen):\n            cycles.append({\n                \"start_index\": current_cycle_start,\n                \"end_index\": i,\n                \"length\": i - current_cycle_start + 1\n            })\n            seen = set()\n            current_cycle_start = i + 1\n            \n    return {\n        \"completed_cycles\": cycles,\n        \"current_cycle_progress\": len(seen) / len(total_range),\n        \"missing_in_current\": sorted(list(total_set - seen))\n    }\n\ndef get_z_score(observed: int, expected: float, std_dev: float) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Standardize the anomaly: (Observed - Expected) / StdDev\"\"\"\n    if std_dev == 0: return 0.0\n    return (observed - expected) / std_dev\n"
  }
}

type: ignore[import-untyped]\n\nfrom engine.adapters import DrawRules\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Result type\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass FrequencyResult:\n    \"\"\"Output of frequency.analyze().\"\"\"\n\n    total_draws: int\n    table: pd.DataFrame          # columns: number, count, frequency, expected_freq, z_score\n    hot: List[int]               # top-N numbers by frequency\n    cold: List[int]              # bottom-N numbers by frequency\n    chi2_statistic: float\n    chi2_p_value: float          # low p → distribution is non-uniform\n\n    def __repr__(self) -> str:\n        return (\n            f\"FrequencyResult(draws={self.total_draws}, \"\n            f\"hot={self.hot[:5]}, cold={self.cold[:5]}, \"\n            f\"chi2_p={self.chi2_p_value:.4f})\"\n        )\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Main function\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef analyze(\n    df: pd.DataFrame,\n    rules: DrawRules,\n    top_n: int = 10,\n    include_bonus: bool = False,\n) -> FrequencyResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute frequency statistics for all numbers in the draw history.\n\n    Parameters\n    ----------\n    df : pd.DataFrame\n        Canonical draw DataFrame (from any LotteryAdapter).\n    rules : DrawRules\n        Lottery rules — used to build the full number universe.\n    top_n : int\n        How many hot/cold numbers to highlight.\n    include_bonus : bool\n        If True, also count bonus ball appearances in the main frequency table.\n\n    Returns\n    -------\n    FrequencyResult\n    \"\"\"\n    if df.empty:\n        raise ValueError(\"DataFrame is empty — fetch data first.\")\n\n    total_draws = len(df)\n    lo, hi = rules.number_range\n    all_numbers = list(range(lo, hi + 1))\n\n    # Flatten all drawn numbers into a single series\n    main_series = pd.Series([n for draw in df[\"numbers\"] for n in draw])\n\n    if include_bonus and \"bonus\" in df.columns:\n        bonus_series = pd.Series([n for draw in df[\"bonus\"] for n in draw if draw])\n        combined = pd.concat([main_series, bonus_series], ignore_index=True)\n    else:\n        combined = main_series\n\n    counts = combined.value_counts().reindex(all_numbers, fill_value=0)\n\n    total_appearances = counts.sum()\n    expected_per_number = total_appearances / len(all_numbers)\n    expected_freq = 1.0 / len(all_numbers)\n\n    freq = counts / total_appearances\n    z_scores = (counts - expected_per_number) / np.sqrt(\n        expected_per_number * (1 - expected_freq)\n    )\n\n    table = pd.DataFrame(\n        {\n            \"number\": all_numbers,\n            \"count\": counts.values,\n            \"frequency\": freq.values.round(6),\n            \"expected_freq\": round(expected_freq, 6),\n            \"z_score\": z_scores.values.round(4),\n        }\n    ).sort_values(\"count\", ascending=False).reset_index(drop=True)\n\n    # Chi-squared goodness-of-fit\n    chi2_stat, chi2_p = stats.chisquare(counts.values)\n\n    hot = table.head(top_n)[\"number\"].tolist()\n    cold = table.tail(top_n)[\"number\"].tolist()\n\n    return FrequencyResult(\n        total_draws=total_draws,\n        table=table,\n        hot=hot,\n        cold=cold,\n        chi2_statistic=float(chi2_stat),\n        chi2_p_value=float(chi2_p),\n    )\n\n\ndef print_report(res: FrequencyResult) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Print a rich-formatted table of the frequency analysis.\"\"\"\n    from rich.console import Console\n    from rich.table import Table\n\n    console = Console()\n    \n    table = Table(\n        title=f\"Frequency Analysis — top/bottom {len(res.hot)}\",\n        box=None,\n        padding=(0, 2)\n    )\n    table.add_column(\"Rank\", style=\"dim\")\n    table.add_column(\"Number\", style=\"bold yellow\")\n    table.add_column(\"Count\", justify=\"right\")\n    table.add_column(\"Freq %\", justify=\"right\")\n    table.add_column(\"Z-score\", justify=\"right\")\n\n    # Show top N and bottom N\n    top_rows = res.table.head(len(res.hot))\n    bottom_rows = res.table.tail(len(res.cold))\n    \n    for i, (_, row) in enumerate(top_rows.iterrows()):\n        table.add_row(\n            str(i + 1),\n            str(int(row[\"number\"])),\n            str(int(row[\"count\"])),\n            f\"{row['frequency']*100:.2f}%\",\n            f\"{row['z_score']:+.3f}\"\n        )\n    \n    table.add_section()\n    \n    for i, (_, row) in enumerate(bottom_rows.iterrows()):\n        rank = res.table.shape[0] - len(res.cold) + i + 1\n        table.add_row(\n            str(rank),\n            str(int(row[\"number\"])),\n            str(int(row[\"count\"])),\n            f\"{row['frequency']*100:.2f}%\",\n            f\"{row['z_score']:+.3f}\"\n        )\n\n    console.print(table)\n    console.print(f\"\\n🔥 [bold]Hot:[/bold]  {res.hot}\")\n    console.print(f\"🧊 [bold]Cold:[/bold] {res.cold}\")\n    \n    p_style = \"green\" if res.chi2_p_value >= 0.05 else \"red\"\n    console.print(f\"\\n[dim]Chi-squared P-value:[/dim] [{p_style}]{res.chi2_p_value:.4f}[/{p_style}]\")\n    if res.chi2_p_value < 0.05:\n        console.print(\"[red]⚠ Significant non-uniformity detected.[/red]\")\n"
  }
}
```

## MODEL ✨

**Developing Tendency Module** I am now focused on building the tendency analysis module. I will utilize the `write_file` tool to create the `engine/modules/tendency.py` file and implement the core logic for this functionality.**Refining Tendency Calculation** I'm refining the tendency calculation logic. Instead of a single result per pattern, I'll compute statistics for each distinct quantity within a pattern, using a `PatternQtyStats` dataclass. This will capture occurrence, average percentage, frequency, last seen details, delay, and trend momentum for each specific quantity value.**Tool Command**:
```json
{
  "name": "write_file",
  "args": {
    "file_path": "/mnt/c/Users/ag/Documents/Obsidian/PKM/Workspace/(P) Prediction Engine/lottery/engine/modules/tendency.py",
    "content": "\"\"\"\nTendency Module — pattern frequency and delay statistics.\n=========================================================\nCalculates Occurrence, Average, Delay, and Trend for structural patterns.\nStrictly decoupled from UI rendering.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom typing import List, Dict, Any, Callable, Optional\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\n\n@dataclass\nclass PatternQtyStats:\n    qty: int\n    occurrence: int\n    average_pct: float\n    avg_frequency: float  # 1 in N\n    last_seen_id: int\n    last_seen_date: str\n    delay: int\n    trend: List[int]      # Occurrence streak in last N draws\n\n@dataclass\nclass TendencyResult:\n    pattern_name: str\n    stats: List[PatternQtyStats]\n    total_draws: int\n\ndef analyze_pattern_tendency(\n    df: pd.DataFrame,\n    pattern_func: Callable[[List[int], Optional[List[int]]], int],\n    name: str,\n    window: int = 20\n) -> TendencyResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Analyze the tendency of a pattern across historical draws.\n    \n    Parameters\n    ----------\n    df : pd.DataFrame\n        Canonical draw DataFrame with 'draw_id', 'draw_date', and 'numbers'.\n    pattern_func : Callable\n        Function that takes (current_numbers, previous_numbers) and returns an int qty.\n    name : str\n        Human-readable name of the pattern.\n    window : int\n        Window size for trend calculation.\n    \"\"\"\n    if df.empty:\n        return TendencyResult(name, [], 0)\n\n    # Calculate quantities for each draw\n    qtys = []\n    prev_numbers = None\n    for _, row in df.iterrows():\n        qty = pattern_func(row[\"numbers\"], prev_numbers)\n        qtys.append(qty)\n        prev_numbers = row[\"numbers\"]\n\n    df_qtys = df.copy()\n    df_qtys[\"qty\"] = qtys\n\n    total_draws = len(df_qtys)\n    unique_qtys = sorted(df_qtys[\"qty\"].unique())\n    \n    results = []\n    for q in unique_qtys:\n        q_mask = df_qtys[\"qty\"] == q\n        occurrence = int(q_mask.sum())\n        average_pct = (occurrence / total_draws) * 100\n        avg_frequency = total_draws / occurrence if occurrence > 0 else 0\n        \n        # Last seen info\n        hits = df_qtys[q_mask]\n        if not hits.empty:\n            last_row = hits.iloc[-1]\n            last_seen_id = int(last_row[\"draw_id\"])\n            last_seen_date = str(last_row[\"draw_date\"])\n            delay = int(df_qtys[\"draw_id\"].iloc[-1] - last_seen_id) # Simplistic delay based on ID difference\n            # Better delay: count of draws since last seen in sequence\n            last_index = hits.index[-1]\n            delay = total_draws - 1 - last_index\n        else:\n            last_seen_id = 0\n            last_seen_date = \"N/A\"\n            delay = total_draws\n\n        # Trend (last N draws)\n        trend = [1 if val == q else 0 for val in qtys[-window:]]\n        \n        results.append(PatternQtyStats(\n            qty=int(q),\n            occurrence=occurrence,\n            average_pct=round(average_pct, 2),\n            avg_frequency=round(avg_frequency, 2),\n            last_seen_id=last_seen_id,\n            last_seen_date=last_seen_date,\n            delay=delay,\n            trend=trend\n        ))\n        \n    return TendencyResult(name, results, total_draws)\n"
  },
  "id": "write_file_1778120957146_0"
}
```

Trend (last N draws)\n        trend = [1 if val == q else 0 for val in qtys[-window:]]\n        \n        results.append(PatternQtyStats(\n            qty=int(q),\n            occurrence=occurrence,\n            average_pct=round(average_pct, 2),\n            avg_frequency=round(avg_frequency, 2),\n            last_seen_id=last_seen_id,\n            last_seen_date=last_seen_date,\n            delay=delay,\n            trend=trend\n        ))\n        \n    return TendencyResult(name, results, total_draws)\n"

    "output": "\"\"\"\nAnalyze Command 📊\n=================\nRun statistical analysis modules or a unified dashboard.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom typing import Annotated, Optional\n\nimport typer\nfrom rich.console import Console\n\nfrom engine.cli.utils import get_adapter, print_command_summary\nfrom engine.modules import frequency, deviation, correlation\n\nconsole = Console()\n\ndef analyze(\n    lottery: Annotated[str, typer.Argument(help=\"Lottery name [Required]\")],\n    module: Annotated[str, typer.Option(\"--module\", \"-m\", help=\"frequency | deviation | correlation | dashboard | summary\")] = \"dashboard\",\n    limit: Annotated[Optional[int], typer.Option(\"--limit\", \"-n\", help=\"Use only the N most recent draws\")] = None,\n    top: Annotated[int, typer.Option(\"--top\", help=\"Number of items to show in tables\")] = 10,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"📊 Run statistical analysis modules or a unified dashboard.\"\"\"\n\n    print_command_summary(\"analyze\", lottery, module=module, limit=limit, top=top)\n\n    with console.status(f\"[bold green]Loading {lottery} data…\"):\n        adapter = get_adapter(lottery)\n        df = adapter.fetch(limit=limit)\n\n    if module == \"frequency\":\n        res = frequency.analyze(df, adapter.rules, top_n=top)\n        frequency.print_report(res)\n    elif module == \"deviation\":\n        res = deviation.analyze(df, adapter.rules, top_n=top)\n        deviation.print_report(res)\n    elif module == \"correlation\":\n        res = correlation.analyze(df, adapter.rules, top_n=top)\n        correlation.print_report(res)\n    elif module == \"dashboard\":\n        # Deferred import to keep help fast\n        from engine.cli.commands.dashboard import render_dashboard\n        render_dashboard(df, adapter.rules, limit=limit or 100)\n    elif module == \"summary\":\n        # Minimalist summary for quick look\n        f_res = frequency.analyze(df, adapter.rules, top_n=5)\n        d_res = deviation.analyze(df, adapter.rules, top_n=5)\n        console.print(f\"\\n[bold]Hot Numbers:[/bold]  {f_res.hot}\")\n        console.print(f\"[bold]Cold Numbers:[/bold] {f_res.cold}\")\n        console.print(f\"[bold]Overdue:[/bold]      {d_res.overdue}\")\n    else:\n        typer.echo(f\"Unknown module '{module}'. Choose: frequency, deviation, correlation, dashboard, summary\")\n        raise typer.Exit(1)\n"
  }
}

Insights Panel\n    f_res = frequency.analyze(df, rules, top_n=5)\n    d_res = deviation.analyze(df_full, rules, top_n=5)\n    \n    insights = insight_mod.get_automated_insights(df_full, rules, limit=limit)\n    \n    # Chi-squared badge\n    p_val = f_res.chi2_p_value\n    p_style = \"green\" if p_val >= 0.05 else \"red\"\n    p_badge = f\"[{p_style}]P-Value: {p_val:.4f}[/{p_style}]\"\n    \n    layout[\"insights\"].update(Panel(\n        \"\\n\".join(insights) + f\"\\n\\n  {p_badge}\", \n        title=\"Automated Insight Ribbon\", \n        border_style=\"cyan\"\n    ))\n\n    # Static content\n    layout[\"header\"].update(Panel(f\"[bold gold1]LottoLogic Terminal Dashboard: {rules.name}[/bold gold1] [dim](Window: last {limit})[/dim]\", border_style=\"gold1\"))\n    layout[\"footer\"].update(Panel(\"[dim]Press Ctrl+C to exit. Data current as of last fetch.[/dim]\", border_style=\"dim\"))\n    \n    # 2. Stats Panel\n    s_table = Table(box=None)\n    s_table.add_column(\"Rank\", style=\"dim\")\n    s_table.add_column(\"Num\", style=\"bold yellow\")\n    s_table.add_column(\"Z-Score\", justify=\"right\")\n    for i, n in enumerate(f_res.hot):\n        row = f_res.table[f_res.table[\"number\"] == n].iloc[0]\n        s_table.add_row(str(i+1), str(n), f\"{row['z_score']:+.2f}\")\n    layout[\"stats\"].update(Panel(s_table, title=\"Top Performers (Z-Score)\"))\n    \n    # 3. Anomalies Panel\n    a_table = Table(box=None)\n    a_table.add_column(\"Num\", style=\"bold red\")\n    a_table.add_column(\"Delay\", justify=\"right\")\n    a_table.add_column(\"Max\", justify=\"right\", style=\"dim\")\n    for n in d_res.streak_free:\n        row = d_res.table[d_res.table[\"number\"] == n].iloc[0]\n        max_d = d_res.gap_stats.get(n, {}).get(\"max\", \"-\")\n        a_table.add_row(str(n), f\"{int(row['draws_since_last'])}d\", str(max_d))\n    layout[\"anomalies\"].update(Panel(a_table, title=\"Overdue vs Max Delay\"))\n\n    # 4. Voids Panel\n    draws_list = df_full[\"numbers\"].tolist()\n    lo_r, hi_r = rules.number_range\n    \n    v_data = analyze_voids(draws_list, cols=rules.board_cols, max_n=hi_r)\n    v_table = Table(box=None)\n    v_table.add_column(\"Sector\", style=\"dim\")\n    v_table.add_column(\"Delay\", justify=\"right\")\n    for r in sorted(v_data[\"row_delays\"].keys()):\n        delay = v_data[\"row_delays\"][r]\n        if delay >= 3:\n            v_table.add_row(f\"Row {r}\", f\"[bold red]{delay}d[/bold red]\")\n    layout[\"voids\"].update(Panel(v_table, title=\"Spatial Voids (Cold Rows)\"))\n\n    # 4.5 Structural Panel\n    last_nums = draws_list[-1]\n    st_table = Table(box=None, show_header=False)\n    \n    ac = f_mod.get_ac_value(last_nums)\n    rs = f_mod.get_root_sum(last_nums)\n    us = f_mod.get_unit_metrics(last_nums)[\"sum\"]\n    \n    st_table.add_row(\"AC Value\", f\"[bold cyan]{ac}[/bold cyan]\")\n    st_table.add_row(\"Root Sum\", f\"[bold yellow]{rs}[/bold yellow]\")\n    st_table.add_row(\"Unit Sum\", f\"[bold magenta]{us}[/bold magenta]\")\n    \n    layout[\"structural\"].update(Panel(st_table, title=\"Structural Harmony\"))\n\n    # 4.6 Clusters & Gaps Panel\n    c_table = Table(box=None, show_header=False)\n    succ = f_mod.get_successive_metrics(last_nums)\n    dist = f_mod.get_distance_metrics(last_nums)\n    \n    c_table.add_row(\"Max Gap\", f\"[bold yellow]{dist['max']}[/bold yellow]\")\n    c_table.add_row(\"Succ. Groups\", f\"[bold cyan]{succ['groups']}[/bold cyan]\")\n    c_table.add_row(\"Spread\", f\"[bold green]{dist['spread']}[/bold green]\")\n    \n    # Gap History Sparkline\n    gap_hist = [f_mod.get_distance_metrics(d)[\"max\"] for d in draws_list[-30:]]\n    gap_spark = \"\"\n    for g in gap_hist:\n        if g > 15: gap_spark += \"[red]▇[/red]\"\n        elif g > 8: gap_spark += \"[yellow]▅[/yellow]\"\n        else: gap_spark += \"[green]▃[/green]\"\n    \n    c_content = Group(\n        c_table,\n        Rule(style=\"dim\"),\n        f\"[dim]Gap History (30d):[/dim]\\n{gap_spark}\"\n    )\n    layout[\"clusters\"].update(Panel(c_content, title=\"Clusters & Gaps\"))\n\n    # 6. Market Overview\n    lo_s, hi_s, mean_s, sigma_s = sum_range.most_probable_range(rules)\n    last_draw = df.iloc[-1]\n    s_val = sum(last_draw[\"numbers\"])\n    \n    cycle_data = analyze_cycle(draws_list, list(range(lo_r, hi_r + 1)))\n    prog = cycle_data['current_cycle_progress']\n\n    last_p = get_pattern_string(last_nums, cols=rules.board_cols, max_n=hi_r)\n    p_history = [get_pattern_string(d, cols=rules.board_cols, max_n=hi_r) for d in draws_list[-100:]]\n    p_count = p_history.count(last_p)\n    \n    sums = [sum(d) for d in df.tail(30)[\"numbers\"]]\n    sum_chart = \"\"\n    for s in sums:\n        if s < lo_s: char = \"[red]▃[/red]\"\n        elif s > hi_s: char = \"[red]▇[/red]\"\n        else: char = \"[green]▅[/green]\"\n        sum_chart += char\n        \n    main_content = (\n        f\"\\n[bold]Latest Draw:[/bold] {sorted(last_draw['numbers'])}\\n\"\n        f\"[bold]Latest Sum:[/bold]  {s_val} ({'IN' if lo_s<=s_val<=hi_s else '[red]OUT[/red]'} band)\\n\\n\"\n        f\"[bold]Row Pattern:[/bold] {last_p} ([dim]{p_count}% rarity[/dim])\\n\\n\"\n        f\"[bold]Sum History (30d):[/bold]\\n{sum_chart}\\n\\n\"\n        f\"[bold]Cycle Progress:[/bold]\\n{bar_chart(prog, width=25)} {prog*100:.1f}%\\n\"\n        f\"  [dim]Remaining in cycle: {cycle_data['missing_in_current'][:10]}...[/dim]\"\n    )\n    layout[\"right\"].update(Panel(main_content, title=\"Market Overview\"))\n    \n    console.print(layout)\n\n"
  }

table.add_column(\"AVG FREQ\", justify=\"right\")\n    table.add_column(\"DELAY\", justify=\"right\")\n    table.add_column(\"TREND (Last 20)\", justify=\"center\")\n\n    for s in res.stats:\n        # High delay alert (Atraso >= 1.5 * Average Frequency)\n        is_alert = s.delay >= (s.avg_frequency * 1.5) and s.avg_frequency > 0\n        row_style = \"bold red\" if is_alert else \"\"\n        \n        # Sparkline-like trend\n        trend_str = \"\".join([\"[green]█[/]\" if v else \"[dim].[/]\" for v in s.trend])\n        \n        table.add_row(\n            str(s.qty),\n            str(s.occurrence),\n            f\"{s.average_pct:.1f}%\",\n            f\"1 in {s.avg_frequency:.1f}\",\n            str(s.delay),\n            trend_str,\n            style=row_style\n        )\n --- (Howard Ideas) Constraints & Filters ---\n            pool_list = [int(n.strip()) for n in pool.split(\",\") if n.strip()] if pool else None\n            key_list  = [int(n.strip()) for n in key.split(\",\") if n.strip()] if key else None\n            filter_list = [f.strip() for f in filters.split(\",\") if f.strip()] if filters else None\n\n            strat.adaptive_window = adaptive_window\n            \n            with console.status(f\"  Running {strategy_label}…\"):\n                result = strat.suggest(\n                    df_sliced, \n                    adapter.rules, \n                    count=count, \n                    temperature=active_temp, \n                    history_limit=None,\n                    pick=pick_count,\n                    pool=pool_list,\n                    key=key_list,\n                    filters=filter_list,\n                    filters_k_of_n=k_of_n\n                )\n            \n            # --- For Dummies Shortcut Tip ---\n            if idx == 0 and len(strat_names) > 1:\n                ui_dummy_block(\"tip\", \"Look at the [bold]Consensus Analysis[/] below to see where multiple strategies agree.\")\n\n            # 3. OSINT Injection\n            if seismic_events:\n                result.metadata[\"seismic_resonance\"] = {\n                    \"count\": len(seismic_events),\n                    \"max_mag\": max(e['properties']['mag'] for e in seismic_events),\n                    \"latest\": seismic_events[0]['properties']['place']\n                }\n            \n            if \"noosphere_intensity\" not in result.metadata:\n                result.metadata.update({\n                    \"noosphere_intensity\": \"Volatile\" if noosphere_val > 0.7 else \"Stable\" if noosphere_val < 0.3 else \"Coherent\",\n                    \"jitter_score\": round(noosphere_val, 3),\n                    \"regime\": \"High Focus\" if noosphere_val > 0.7 else \"Default Resonance\"\n                })\n            \n            if \"phase_name\" not in result.metadata:\n                from engine.strategies.fun.moon_phase import phase_name\n                result.metadata.update({\n                    \"phase_name\": phase_name(draw_date_obj),\n                    \"phase_ratio\": p_ratio,\n                    \"moon_distance\": \"Perigee\" if d_ratio < 0.2 else \"Apogee\" if d_ratio > 0.8 else \"Neutral\",\n                    \"tidal_intensity\": \"high\" if tide > 0.66 else \"low\" if tide < 0.33 else \"normal\"\n                })\n            \n            if \"kp\" not in result.metadata:\n                result.metadata.update({\n                    \"kp\": round(kp_val, 1),\n                    \"intensity\": \"storm\" if kp_val >= 5 else \"unsettled\" if kp_val >= 3 else \"quiet\"\n                })\n\n            if entropy_boost > 0:\n                result.metadata[\"chaos_boost\"] = round(entropy_boost, 2)\n                \n            all_results.append(result)\n            _print_single_result(result, strategy_label, rules=adapter.rules, df=df_full, top_scored=top, strat_obj=strat, explain=explain)\n\n        except Exception as exc:\n            console.print(f\"[red]Strategy failed: {exc}[/red]\")\n            failed.append(name)\n            continue\n\n    if len(all_results) > 1:\n        _print_consensus(all_results)\n\n    if learn:\n        _print_learn_section()\n\n    if failed:\n        console.print(f\"\\n[yellow]⚠  Skipped: {', '.join(failed)}[/yellow]\")\n\ndef _print_single_result(result, label, rules, df, top_scored, strat_obj, explain: bool = False):\n    \"\"\"Prints formatted output for a single strategy suggestion.\"\"\"\n    from engine.cli.utils import ui_panel, ui_table, ui_dummy_block, Theme\n    import numpy as np\n\n    # 1. Metadata Summary Line\n\n    meta = []\n    if \"phase_name\" in result.metadata: meta.append(f\"🌕 {result.metadata['phase_name']}\")\n    if \"kp\" in result.metadata: meta.append(f\"☀️ Kp {result.metadata['kp']}\")\n    if \"chaos_boost\" in result.metadata: meta.append(f\"🌀 Boost +{result.metadata['chaos_boost']}\")\n    subtitle = \" | \".join(meta)\n\n    # 2. Main Ticket Content\n    content = []\n    for i, ticket in enumerate(result.tickets):\n        t_str = \", \".join(map(str, sorted(ticket)))\n        t_sum = sum(ticket)\n        \n        # Historical win context\n        win_counts = {tier: 0 for tier in rules.prize_tiers}\n        for draw_nums in df[\"numbers\"]:\n            matches = len(set(ticket) & set(draw_nums))\n            if matches in win_counts: win_counts[matches] += 1\n        win_info = \" | \".join(f\"M{k}: {v}\" for k, v in sorted(win_counts.items()) if v > 0)\n        \n        content.append(f\"[bold {Theme.SECONDARY}]Ticket {i+1}:[/] {t_str} [dim](sum={t_sum})[/]\")\n        if win_info:\n            content.append(f\"   [dim]⌞ Hist Wins: {win_info}[/]\")\n\n    # 3. Top Scored Table (Evidence)\n    evidence = None\n    if explain:\n        # Feature 5: score entropy (concentration)\n        from engine.cli.utils import calculate_shannon_entropy\n        ent = calculate_shannon_entropy(result.scores)\n        \n        # Max theoretical entropy for this pool size\n        n_items = len(result.scores)\n        max_ent = np.log(n_items)\n        concentration = 1.0 - (ent / (max_ent or 1.0))\n        \n        conc_label = \"diffuse\" if concentration < 0.1 else \"normal\" if concentration < 0.25 else \"concentrated\"\n        \n        evidence = ui_table(columns=[\"Rank\", \"Number\", \"Score\"])\n        sorted_scores = sorted(result.scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:top_scored]\n        for i, (num, score) in enumerate(sorted_scores):\n            evidence.add_row(str(i+1), f\"[bold]{num}[/]\", f\"{score:.4f}\")\n        \n        rprint(f\" [dim]⌞ Score Entropy: {ent:.4f} ({conc_label})[/]\")\n    rprint(ui_panel(\n        Group(\"\\n\".join(content), \"\\n\", ui_panel(evidence, title=\"Statistical Evidence\", style=\"bright_black\") if evidence else \"\"),\n        title=f\"{label} PREDICTION\",\n        subtitle=subtitle,\n        style=Theme.PRIMARY\n    ))\n\n    # Evidence Panel (from v7.0)\n    _print_evidence_panel(result.metadata)\n\n    # Explain Panel: top contributing features\n    if explain:\n        _print_explain_panel(result, rules, df)\n\ndef _print_evidence_panel(metadata: dict):\n    \"\"\"Draw the fancy 'OSINT' evidence panel.\"\"\"\n    from rich.panel import Panel\n    evidence = []\n    \n    if \"phase_name\" in metadata:\n        evidence.append(f\"🌕 Moon phase: {metadata['phase_name']} ({metadata['phase_ratio']*100:.1f}% through cycle) | Distance: {metadata['moon_distance']} | Tide: {metadata['tidal_intensity']}\")\n        \n    if \"kp\" in metadata:\n        evidence.append(f\"☀️ Solar Weather: {metadata['intensity']} (Planetary K-index: {metadata['kp']})\")\n        \n    if \"jitter_score\" in metadata:\n        evidence.append(f\"🧠 Noosphere Jitter: {metadata['noosphere_intensity']} (Jitter Score: {metadata['jitter_score']}) | Regime: {metadata['regime']}\")\n        \n    if \"seismic_resonance\" in metadata:\n        s = metadata[\"seismic_resonance\"]\n        evidence.append(f\"🫨 Seismic Resonance: {'Active' if s['count'] > 0 else 'Stable'} ({s['count']} recent tremors, max mag: {s['max_mag']})\")\n\n    if \"chaos_boost\" in metadata:\n        evidence.append(f\"🌀 Chaos Entropy Boost: +{metadata['chaos_boost']} applied to sampling temperature\")\n\n    if evidence:\n        console.print(Panel(\"\\n\".join(evidence), title=\"Correlation Evidence\", border_style=\"dim\"))\n\n\ndef _print_explain_panel(result, rules, df) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Explain panel: show top-3 contributing features for the prediction.\"\"\"\n    import numpy as np\n    from rich.panel import Panel\n    from scipy import stats\n\n    lines = []\n\n    # Feature 1: top-scoring numbers and their score percentile\n    sorted_scores = sorted(result.scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n    if sorted_scores:\n        top3 = sorted_scores[:3]\n        for rank, (num, score) in enumerate(top3, 1):\n            pct = score / (sorted_scores[0][1] or 1.0) * 100\n            lines.append(f\"Feature {rank}: Number [bold]{num}[/bold]  score={score:.4f} ({pct:.0f}% of max)\")\n\n    # Feature 4: Chi² regime over last 30 draws\n    try:\n        lo, hi = rules.number_range\n        pool_size = hi - lo + 1\n        flat = [n for d in df.tail(30)[\"numbers\"] for n in d]\n        from collections import Counter\n        counts = Counter(flat)\n        observed = np.array([counts.get(n, 0) for n in range(lo, hi + 1)], dtype=float)\n        expected = np.full(pool_size, observed.sum() / pool_size)\n        chi2, p_val = stats.chisquare(observed, expected)\n        regime = \"non-uniform [red](possible structure)[/red]\" if p_val < 0.05 else \"uniform [green](fair / random)[/green]\"\n        lines.append(f\"Feature 4: 30-draw regime — χ²={chi2:.2f}  p={p_val:.4f}  → {regime}\")\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n\n    # Feature 5: score entropy (concentration)\n    from engine.cli.utils import calculate_shannon_entropy\n    ent = calculate_shannon_entropy(result.scores)\n    max_ent = float(np.log(len(result.scores)) if result.scores else 1.0)\n    concentration = 1.0 - ent / (max_ent or 1.0)\n    conc_label = \"focused\" if concentration > 0.3 else \"diffuse\"\n    lines.append(f\"Feature 5: Score entropy={ent:.4f}  concentration={concentration:.2%} ({conc_label})\")\n\n    if lines:\n        console.print(Panel(\"\\n\".join(lines), title=\"🔍 Explain — Top Contributing Features\", border_style=\"blue\"))\n\n\ndef _print_consensus(results: list[Any]) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Prints where multiple strategies agree on numbers (consensus across strategies).\"\"\"\n    # Using TUI Design Language\n    from engine.cli.utils import ui_panel, Theme\n    from collections import Counter\n    \n    counter = Counter()\n    for res in results:\n        for ticket in res.tickets:\n            for n in ticket:\n                counter[n] += 1\n                \n    if not counter: return\n    \n    # 1. Consensus Content\n    lines = []\n    strategy_count = len(results)\n    \n    for num, votes in counter.most_common(15):\n        # We'll just use a simple bar for now as per design language\n        bar = \"█\" * int(votes) + \"░\" * (max(0, strategy_count - votes))\n        lines.append(f\"   [bold yellow]{num:>2}[/]  {bar}  [dim]{votes} votes[/]\")\n        \n    high_conv = [n for n, v in counter.items() if v >= max(2, strategy_count // 2 + 1)]\n    \n    footer = \"\"\n    if high_conv:\n        footer = f\"\\n[bold green]⭐ High Conviction:[/] {sorted(high_conv)}\"\n\n    # 2. Final Render\n    rprint(ui_panel(\n        Group(\"\\n\".join(lines), footer),\n        title=\"CONSENSUS ANALYSIS\",\n        style=Theme.SECONDARY\n    ))\n\n\ndef _print_evidence_panel(metadata: dict):\n    \"\"\"Prints additional environmental context.\"\"\"\n    # (Future: integrate with ui_panel)\n    pass\n\n\ndef _print_learn_section() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Prints educational take-away.\"\"\"\n    console.print()\n    console.rule(\"[bold magenta]LEARN — How to use these results[/bold magenta]\")\n    console.print(\"  • Look for [bold yellow]Convergence[/bold yellow]: Numbers that multiple strategies agree on.\")\n    console.print(\"  • Respect [bold green]Harmony[/bold green]: The engine auto-filters for sum-range and parity balance.\")\n    console.print(\"  • Play [bold cyan]Diversified[/bold cyan]: Use different strategy tiers (Statistical, ML, Esoteric).\")\n"
  }
}
