Inherits    suggest(...)     from BaseStrategy     (sampling + temperature)\n\nTemperature-based sampling (from the deep-learning literature) controls\nhow deterministic vs. exploratory the ticket generation is:\n  temperature = 0   → deterministic: always pick the top-N numbers\n  temperature = 1   → sample proportional to scores\n  temperature > 1   → more exploratory / random\n  temperature < 1   → sharper, more confident picks\n\nEnsemble is trivial: pass multiple strategy names separated by commas to the\nCLI and a DynamicEnsemble averages their score dicts automatically.\n\nStrategy tiers\n--------------\n  statistical/   pure stats, no extra deps  (markov, bayesian, monte_carlo, weighted, pattern)\n  ml/            scikit-learn required       (logistic, random_forest, ensemble)\n  deep/          torch required              (transformer, lstm_gru)\n  fun/           entertainment / absurdity   (numerology, moon_phase, weather)\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport random\nfrom abc import ABC, abstractmethod\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\n\nfrom engine.adapters import DrawRules\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Result type\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass SuggestionResult:\n    \"\"\"Returned by every strategy's suggest() method.\"\"\"\n\n    strategy_name: str\n    tickets: list[list[int]]        # each ticket is a sorted list of numbers\n    scores: dict[int, float]        # per-number score 0–1\n    confidence: float = 0.0         # strategy's self-reported confidence\n    metadata: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\n\n    def __repr__(self) -> str:\n        return (\n            f\"SuggestionResult(strategy={self.strategy_name!r}, \"\n            f\"tickets={len(self.tickets)}, confidence={self.confidence:.3f})\"\n        )\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Base class\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass BaseStrategy(ABC):\n    \"\"\"\n    Abstract base for all prediction strategies.\n\n    Subclasses implement score(); suggest() is provided for free.\n\n    Class-level attributes\n    ----------------------\n    name                 : str   — short identifier used in the CLI, e.g. \"markov\".\n    description          : str   — one-line description shown in ``lottery strategies``.\n    requires_history     : int   — minimum number of draws needed before the strategy\n                                   is meaningful (default: 30).\n    tier                 : str   — one of \"statistical\" | \"ml\" | \"deep\" | \"fun\".\n    recent_matches_count : int   — how many recent matched draws to include in the\n                                   ``recent_matches`` metadata list returned by\n                                   correlation strategies (moon_phase, weather).\n                                   Override per strategy or pass ``--recent-matches``\n                                   via the CLI (default: 5).\n    top_numbers_count    : int   — how many top-scoring numbers to include in the\n                                   ``top_numbers`` metadata list returned by\n                                   correlation strategies.  Also drives the display\n                                   in ``lottery suggest``.  Pass ``--top-numbers``\n                                   via the CLI to override (default: 10).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    name: str                        # short identifier used in CLI  e.g. \"markov\"\n    description: str                 # one-line description for lottery list\n    requires_history:     int = 30   # minimum draws needed before strategy is meaningful\n    tier:                 str = \"statistical\"   # \"statistical\" | \"ml\" | \"deep\" | \"fun\"\n    recent_matches_count: int = 5    # rows surfaced in metadata[\"recent_matches\"]\n    top_numbers_count:    int = 10   # rows surfaced in metadata[\"top_numbers\"]\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Must override\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    @abstractmethod\n    def score(self, df: pd.DataFrame, rules: DrawRules, **kwargs: Any) -> dict[int, float]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Assign a score 0–1 to every number in the lottery pool.\n        Higher score → the strategy thinks this number is more likely to appear.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        df    : canonical draw DataFrame (sorted ascending by draw_id)\n        rules : DrawRules for the target lottery\n\n        Returns\n        -------\n        dict mapping every number in range(lo, hi+1) to a float in [0, 1].\n        Scores need not sum to 1 — normalisation happens in suggest().\n        \"\"\"\n        ...\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Provided\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    def is_harmonious(self, ticket: list[int], rules: DrawRules, filters: list[str] | None = None, k_of_n: int | None = None, **kwargs: Any) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"\n        Structural Harmony Check with Meta-Logic (K-of-N Fault Tolerance).\n        \"\"\"\n        n_picked = len(ticket)\n        lo, hi = rules.number_range\n        expected_avg = (lo + hi) / 2\n        \n        active_list: list[str]\n        if filters is None:\n            active_list = [\"sum_range\", \"parity\", \"breadth\"]\n        elif \"balanced\" in filters:\n            expanded = [f for f in filters if f != \"balanced\"]\n            expanded += [\"sum_range\", \"parity\", \"breadth\"]\n            active_list = expanded\n        else:\n            active_list = filters\n            \n        VALID_FILTERS = {\n            \"sum_range\", \"parity\", \"breadth\", \"no_consecutive\", \n            \"no_arithmetic\", \"birthday_bias\", \"no_calendar_cluster\",\n            \"no_same_last_digit\", \"repeat_rate\", \"parity_balance\",\n            \"high_low_balance\", \"sum_harmonic\", \"prime_count\",\n            \"ac_value\", \"unit_sum\", \"successive_groups\", \"root_sum\",\n            \"hot_cold\", \"no_historical_dupes\", \"no_multiples_cluster\",\n            \"pos_1_low\", \"pos_last_high\", \"mixed_parity_digits\",\n            \"digit_space_123\", \"successive_end_units\", \"density\"\n        }\n\n        if \"all\" in active_list:\n            active = VALID_FILTERS\n        else:\n            active = set(active_list)\n            unknown = active - VALID_FILTERS\n            if unknown:\n                import logging\n                logging.warning(f\"Unknown filters requested: {unknown}. These will be ignored.\")\n        \n        results = []\n\n        # Helper to record results for K-of-N\n        def check(cond: bool):\n            results.append(cond)\n\n        # 26. Density Ratio (Frame/Center)\n        if \"density\" in active:\n            density_param = kwargs.get(\"density\")\n            if density_param:\n                try:\n                    f_target, c_target = map(int, density_param.split(\":\"))\n                    from engine.modules.patterns import get_frame_center_logic\n                    cols = 5 if \"lotofacil\" in rules.name.lower() else 10\n                    max_n = rules.number_range[1]\n                    res = get_frame_center_logic(ticket, cols=cols, max_n=max_n)\n                    check(len(res[\"frame\"]) == f_target and len(res[\"center\"]) == c_target)\n                except ValueError:\n                    check(True) # Fallback if unparseable\n\n        # 1. Dynamic Sum Range\n        if \"sum_range\" in active:\n            t_sum = sum(ticket)\n            ideal_sum = expected_avg * n_picked\n            tolerance = 0.3 if n_picked == rules.pick_count else 0.4\n            check(ideal_sum * (1.0 - tolerance) <= t_sum <= ideal_sum * (1.0 + tolerance))\n\n        # 2. Dynamic Parity Check (Odd/Even)\n        if \"parity\" in active:\n            odds = sum(1 for n in ticket if n % 2 != 0)\n            if n_picked >= 10:\n                check(0.2 * n_picked <= odds <= 0.8 * n_picked)\n            else:\n                check(0 < odds < n_picked)\n            \n        # 3. Breadth Check (Decades)\n        if \"breadth\" in active:\n            decades = {n // 10 for n in ticket}\n            min_decades = 3 if n_picked < 10 else 4\n            check(len(decades) >= min_decades)\n\n        # 4. (Howard Idea 7) No consecutive runs (4+)\n        if \"no_consecutive\" in active:\n            sorted_t = sorted(ticket)\n            consecutive = 1\n            max_c = 1\n            for i in range(len(sorted_t) - 1):\n                if sorted_t[i+1] == sorted_t[i] + 1:\n                    consecutive += 1\n                else:\n                    max_c = max(max_c, consecutive)\n                    consecutive = 1\n            max_c = max(max_c, consecutive)\n            check(max_c < 4)\n\n        # 5. (Howard Idea 7) No arithmetic sequences\n        if \"no_arithmetic\" in active and n_picked >= 3:\n            sorted_t = sorted(ticket)\n            is_arith = False\n            for i in range(len(sorted_t) - 2):\n                diff = sorted_t[i+1] - sorted_t[i]\n                if diff > 1: # consecutive already handled\n                    if sorted_t[i+2] == sorted_t[i+1] + diff:\n                        is_arith = True\n                        break\n            check(not is_arith)\n\n        # 6. (Howard Idea 9) Birthday Bias (Calendar Cluster)\n        if \"birthday_bias\" in active or \"no_calendar_cluster\" in active:\n            birthdays = sum(1 for n in ticket if n <= 31)\n            limit = 4 if n_picked <= 6 else int(n_picked * 0.8)\n            check(not (birthdays >= limit and n_picked <= 31))\n\n        # 7. (Howard Idea 6) No same last digit\n        if \"no_same_last_digit\" in active:\n            last_digits = [n % 10 for n in ticket]\n            from collections import Counter\n            counts = Counter(last_digits)\n            threshold = 3 if n_picked <= 10 else 5\n            check(not any(c >= threshold for c in counts.values()))\n\n        # 8. Historical Repeat Rate (from last draw)\n        if \"repeat_rate\" in active and \"last_draw\" in kwargs:\n            last_nums = set(kwargs[\"last_draw\"])\n            repeats = len(set(ticket) & last_nums)\n            max_repeats = max(2, int(rules.pick_count * 0.4))\n            check(repeats <= max_repeats)\n\n        # 9. (Smart Luck) Parity Balance\n        if \"parity_balance\" in active:\n            odds = sum(1 for n in ticket if n % 2 != 0)\n            evens = n_picked - odds\n            limit = 2 if n_picked <= 6 else 3\n            check(abs(odds - evens) <= limit)\n\n        # 10. (Smart Luck) High/Low Balance\n        if \"high_low_balance\" in active:\n            highs = sum(1 for n in ticket if n > expected_avg)\n            lows = n_picked - highs\n            limit = 2 if n_picked <= 6 else 3\n            check(abs(highs - lows) <= limit)\n                \n        # 11. (Smart Luck) Sum Harmonic\n        if \"sum_harmonic\" in active:\n            from engine.modules import sum_range\n            _, _, mean, sigma = sum_range.most_probable_range(rules)\n            t_sum = sum(ticket)\n            check(abs(t_sum - mean) <= sigma)\n\n        # --- SamLotto Power Pack (Advanced Filters) ---\n        from engine.modules import filters as f_mod\n        \n        if \"prime_count\" in active:\n            p_count = f_mod.get_prime_count(ticket)\n            check(1 <= p_count <= (3 if n_picked <= 6 else 7))\n\n        if \"ac_value\" in active:\n            ac = f_mod.get_ac_value(ticket)\n            check(ac >= (7 if n_picked == 6 else 10))\n\n        if \"unit_sum\" in active:\n            u_sum = f_mod.get_unit_metrics(ticket)[\"sum\"]\n            check((n_picked * 2) <= u_sum <= (n_picked * 7))\n\n        if \"successive_groups\" in active:\n            groups = f_mod.get_successive_metrics(ticket)[\"groups\"]\n            check(groups <= 2 if n_picked <= 6 else True)\n\n        if \"root_sum\" in active:\n            r_sum = f_mod.get_root_sum(ticket)\n            check(r_sum not in (1, 9))\n\n        if \"hot_cold\" in active and \"df\" in kwargs:\n            df_hist = kwargs[\"df\"]\n            flat = [n for d in df_hist.tail(30)[\"numbers\"] for n in d]\n            from collections import Counter\n            counts = Counter(flat)\n            n_pool = rules.number_range[1] - rules.number_range[0] + 1\n            hot_set = set(sorted(counts, key=counts.get, reverse=True)[:n_pool // 4])\n            hot_count = len(set(ticket) & hot_set)\n            check(hot_count >= (2 if n_picked <= 6 else 4))\n\n        if \"no_historical_dupes\" in active and \"df\" in kwargs:\n            df_hist = kwargs[\"df\"]\n            t_set = set(ticket)\n            is_dupe = any(t_set == set(prev) for prev in df_hist[\"numbers\"])\n            check(not is_dupe)\n\n        if \"no_multiples_cluster\" in active:\n            m7 = f_mod.get_multiples_count(ticket, 7)\n            m10 = f_mod.get_multiples_count(ticket, 10)\n            check(m7 < 3 and m10 < 3)\n\n        # 20. Mixed Parity Digits\n        if \"mixed_parity_digits\" in active:\n            p_metrics = f_mod.get_digit_parity_pairs(ticket)\n            # Most random tickets have a mix. Avoid tickets with 0 mixed parity numbers.\n            check(p_metrics[\"mixed\"] >= 1)\n\n        # 21. Restricted Digit Space (1-2-3)\n        if \"digit_space_123\" in active:\n            d_count = f_mod.get_restricted_digit_space_count(ticket, {0, 1, 2, 3})\n            # Avoid tickets where all numbers are from this restricted space (unlikely)\n            check(d_count < n_picked)\n\n        # 22. Successive End Units (Modular Succession)\n        if \"successive_end_units\" in active:\n            s_end = f_mod.get_successive_end_units(ticket)\n            # Avoid long diagonal patterns (4+)\n            check(s_end < 4)\n\n        # --- POSITIONAL PHYSICS (Ordered Vector Distribution) ---\n        sorted_t = sorted(ticket)\n        if \"pos_1_low\" in active:\n            check(sorted_t[0] <= (hi - lo) * 0.25 + lo)\n        \n        if \"pos_last_high\" in active:\n            check(sorted_t[-1] >= hi - (hi - lo) * 0.25)\n\n        # Meta-Logic Evaluation\n        if not results: return True\n        if k_of_n:\n            return sum(1 for r in results if r) >= k_of_n\n        else:\n            return all(results)\n\n    def suggest(\n        self,\n        df: pd.DataFrame,\n        rules: DrawRules,\n        count: int = 1,\n        temperature: float = 1.0,\n        history_limit: int | None = None,\n        seed: int | None = None,\n        pick: int | None = None,\n        pool: list[int] | None = None,\n        key: list[int] | None = None,\n        filters: list[str] | None = None,\n        filters_k_of_n: int | None = None,\n        **kwargs: Any,\n    ) -> SuggestionResult:\n        \"\"\"\n        Generate `count` ticket suggestions using temperature-based sampling.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        count          : number of distinct tickets to generate\n        temperature    : sampling sharpness (0=deterministic, 1=proportional, >1=noisy)\n        history_limit  : if set, only use the most recent N draws for scoring\n        seed           : optional random seed for intentional/personalized synchronicity\n        pick           : optional pick count override (for multiple bets)\n        pool           : restrict possible numbers to this subset\n        key            : mandatory numbers to include in every ticket\n        filters        : list of structural filters to apply\n        filters_k_of_n : pass if ticket satisfies at least K of the active filters\n        kwargs         : strategy-specific parameters\n        \"\"\"\n        # Default to standard pick count from rules if not overridden\n        target_pick = pick if pick is not None else rules.pick_count\n\n        # 0. Adaptive Window Sweep (v8.0)\n        final_history_limit = history_limit\n        adaptive = getattr(self, \"adaptive_window\", False)\n        if adaptive and len(df) > 50:\n            best_limit = None\n            best_precision = -1.0\n            # Test 3 regimes: Fast (20), Medium (50), Deep (All)\n            for test_limit in [20, 50, len(df)]:\n                best_limit = test_limit\n            final_history_limit = best_limit\n\n        # Apply recency bias limit if requested\n        if final_history_limit is not None and final_history_limit > 0:\n            df = df.tail(final_history_limit)\n\n        if len(df) < self.requires_history:\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"{self.name} requires >= {self.requires_history} draws, \"\n                f\"got {len(df)}.\"\n            )\n            \n        # Warning if running close to the data floor\n        if self.requires_history > 0 and len(df) < (self.requires_history * 1.2):\n            import logging\n            logging.warning(\n                f\"Strategy '{self.name}' is running near its minimum data floor \"\n                f\"({len(df)}/{self.requires_history} draws). Results may be volatile.\"\n            )\n\n        # Only pass kwargs that belong to score\n        score_kwargs = {k: v for k, v in kwargs.items() if k not in [\"filters\", \"k_of_n\", \"density\", \"pool\", \"key\"]}\n        scores = self.score(df, rules, **score_kwargs)\n\n        # Normalise to [0, 1]\n        lo, hi = rules.number_range\n        \n        # --- (Howard Idea 2) Pool Constraint ---\n        if pool:\n            active_numbers = sorted(list(set(pool) & set(range(lo, hi + 1))))\n            if not active_numbers:\n                raise ValueError(\"Provided --pool has no valid numbers for this lottery.\")\n        else:\n            active_numbers = list(range(lo, hi + 1))\n\n        max_s = max(scores.values()) if scores else 1.0\n        min_s = min(scores.values()) if scores else 0.0\n        rng = max_s - min_s or 1.0\n        \n        # Only normalise numbers that are in our active pool\n        normed = {n: (scores.get(n, 0.0) - min_s) / rng for n in active_numbers}\n\n        # 4. Geometric Singularity (v7.0)\n        # Identify top numbers and boost their 'Dodecahedron Neighbors'\n        from engine.modules.geometry import get_dodecahedron_neighbors\n        top_ns = sorted(normed.keys(), key=normed.get, reverse=True)[:3]\n        for top_n in top_ns:\n            neighbors = get_dodecahedron_neighbors(top_n, max_n=hi)\n            for nb in neighbors:\n                if nb in normed:\n                    # Boost neighbors by 15% of the top number's score\n                    normed[nb] = min(1.0, normed[nb] + (normed[top_n] * 0.15))\n\n        # Initialize seeded random if provided\n        import random\n        local_rng = random.Random(seed) if seed is not None else random.Random()\n        \n        # --- (Howard Idea 3) Key Number Handling ---\n        mandatory_keys = []\n        if key:\n            mandatory_keys = sorted(list(set(key) & set(active_numbers)))\n            if len(mandatory_keys) > target_pick:\n                raise ValueError(f\"Too many mandatory keys ({len(mandatory_keys)}) for pick-{target_pick}\")\n        \n        n_to_sample = target_pick - len(mandatory_keys)\n\n        tickets: list[list[int]] = []\n        # Max attempts to find harmonious tickets\n        max_attempts = count * 20\n        attempts = 0\n        \n        while len(tickets) < count and attempts < max_attempts:\n            attempts += 1\n            \n            # --- (Howard Idea 3) Key Number Injection ---\n            remaining_normed = {n: v for n, v in normed.items() if n not in mandatory_keys}\n            if n_to_sample > 0:\n                sampled = _weighted_sample(list(remaining_normed.keys()), remaining_normed, n_to_sample, temperature, rng=local_rng)\n                ticket = sorted(list(sampled) + mandatory_keys)\n            else:\n                ticket = sorted(mandatory_keys)\n            \n            # (v9.0) Atmospheric Muon Flux: Simulate a Single-Event Upset (bit flip)\n            # A cosmic ray (muon) has a very small chance (e.g., 1 in 1000) of striking \n            # the simulation memory and flipping a bit, mutating one number.\n            muon_hit = False\n            if local_rng.random() < 0.005:  # 0.5% chance per ticket for fun, realistically much lower\n                muon_hit = True\n                flip_idx = local_rng.randint(0, target_pick - 1)\n                \n                # 'Flip' the number: e.g. bitwise XOR with 1, 2, 4, 8, 16 or 32\n                bit_flip = 1 << local_rng.randint(0, 5) \n                mutated = ticket[flip_idx] ^ bit_flip\n                \n                # Keep it in range and unique\n                if mutated < lo: mutated = lo\n                if mutated > hi: mutated = hi\n                \n                # If collision, just nudge it\n                while mutated in ticket:\n                    mutated = ((mutated - lo + 1) % (hi - lo + 1)) + lo\n                    \n                ticket[flip_idx] = mutated\n                ticket.sort()\n            "
  }
}

These will be ignored.\")\n        \n        results = []\n\n        # Helper to record results for K-of-N\n        def check(cond: bool):\n            results.append(cond)\n\n        # 26. Density Ratio (Frame/Center)\n        if \"density\" in active:\n            density_param = kwargs.get(\"density\")\n            if density_param:\n                try:\n                    f_target, c_target = map(int, density_param.split(\":\"))\n                    from engine.modules.patterns import get_frame_center_logic\n                    cols = 5 if \"lotofacil\" in rules.name.lower() else 10\n                    max_n = rules.number_range[1]\n                    res = get_frame_center_logic(ticket, cols=cols, max_n=max_n)\n                    check(len(res[\"frame\"]) == f_target and len(res[\"center\"]) == c_target)\n                except ValueError:\n                    check(True) # Fallback if unparseable\n\n        # 1. Dynamic Sum Range\n        if \"sum_range\" in active:\n            t_sum = sum(ticket)\n            ideal_sum = expected_avg * n_picked\n            tolerance = 0.3 if n_picked == rules.pick_count else 0.4\n            check(ideal_sum * (1.0 - tolerance) <= t_sum <= ideal_sum * (1.0 + tolerance))\n\n        # 2. Dynamic Parity Check (Odd/Even)\n        if \"parity\" in active:\n            odds = sum(1 for n in ticket if n % 2 != 0)\n            if n_picked >= 10:\n                check(0.2 * n_picked <= odds <= 0.8 * n_picked)\n            else:\n                check(0 < odds < n_picked)\n            \n        # 3. Breadth Check (Decades)\n        if \"breadth\" in active:\n            decades = {n // 10 for n in ticket}\n            min_decades = 3 if n_picked < 10 else 4\n            check(len(decades) >= min_decades)\n\n        # 4. (Howard Idea 7) No consecutive runs (4+)\n        if \"no_consecutive\" in active:\n            sorted_t = sorted(ticket)\n            consecutive = 1\n            max_c = 1\n            for i in range(len(sorted_t) - 1):\n                if sorted_t[i+1] == sorted_t[i] + 1:\n                    consecutive += 1\n                else:\n                    max_c = max(max_c, consecutive)\n                    consecutive = 1\n            max_c = max(max_c, consecutive)\n            check(max_c < 4)\n\n        # 5. (Howard Idea 7) No arithmetic sequences\n        if \"no_arithmetic\" in active and n_picked >= 3:\n            sorted_t = sorted(ticket)\n            is_arith = False\n            for i in range(len(sorted_t) - 2):\n                diff = sorted_t[i+1] - sorted_t[i]\n                if diff > 1: # consecutive already handled\n                    if sorted_t[i+2] == sorted_t[i+1] + diff:\n                        is_arith = True\n                        break\n            check(not is_arith)\n\n        # 6. (Howard Idea 9) Birthday Bias (Calendar Cluster)\n        if \"birthday_bias\" in active or \"no_calendar_cluster\" in active:\n            birthdays = sum(1 for n in ticket if n <= 31)\n            limit = 4 if n_picked <= 6 else int(n_picked * 0.8)\n            check(not (birthdays >= limit and n_picked <= 31))\n\n        # 7. (Howard Idea 6) No same last digit\n        if \"no_same_last_digit\" in active:\n            last_digits = [n % 10 for n in ticket]\n            from collections import Counter\n            counts = Counter(last_digits)\n            threshold = 3 if n_picked <= 10 else 5\n            check(not any(c >= threshold for c in counts.values()))\n\n        # 8. Historical Repeat Rate (from last draw)\n        if \"repeat_rate\" in active and \"last_draw\" in kwargs:\n            last_nums = set(kwargs[\"last_draw\"])\n            repeats = len(set(ticket) & last_nums)\n            max_repeats = max(2, int(rules.pick_count * 0.4))\n            check(repeats <= max_repeats)\n\n        # 9. (Smart Luck) Parity Balance\n        if \"parity_balance\" in active:\n            odds = sum(1 for n in ticket if n % 2 != 0)\n            evens = n_picked - odds\n            limit = 2 if n_picked <= 6 else 3\n            check(abs(odds - evens) <= limit)\n\n        # 10. (Smart Luck) High/Low Balance\n        if \"high_low_balance\" in active:\n            highs = sum(1 for n in ticket if n > expected_avg)\n            lows = n_picked - highs\n            limit = 2 if n_picked <= 6 else 3\n            check(abs(highs - lows) <= limit)\n                \n        # 11. (Smart Luck) Sum Harmonic\n        if \"sum_harmonic\" in active:\n            from engine.modules import sum_range\n            _, _, mean, sigma = sum_range.most_probable_range(rules)\n            t_sum = sum(ticket)\n            check(abs(t_sum - mean) <= sigma)\n\n        # --- SamLotto Power Pack (Advanced Filters) ---\n        from engine.modules import filters as f_mod\n        \n        if \"prime_count\" in active:\n            p_count = f_mod.get_prime_count(ticket)\n            check(1 <= p_count <= (3 if n_picked <= 6 else 7))\n\n        if \"ac_value\" in active:\n            ac = f_mod.get_ac_value(ticket)\n            check(ac >= (7 if n_picked == 6 else 10))\n\n        if \"unit_sum\" in active:\n            u_sum = f_mod.get_unit_metrics(ticket)[\"sum\"]\n            check((n_picked * 2) <= u_sum <= (n_picked * 7))\n\n        if \"successive_groups\" in active:\n            groups = f_mod.get_successive_metrics(ticket)[\"groups\"]\n            check(groups <= 2 if n_picked <= 6 else True)\n\n        if \"root_sum\" in active:\n            r_sum = f_mod.get_root_sum(ticket)\n            check(r_sum not in (1, 9))\n\n        if \"hot_cold\" in active and \"df\" in kwargs:\n            df_hist = kwargs[\"df\"]"
  }

lo + 1) % (hi - lo + 1)) + lo\n                    \n                ticket[flip_idx] = mutated\n                ticket.sort()\n            \n            # If we already have this ticket, skip\n            if ticket in tickets:\n                continue\n                \n            # Filter check: only apply when filters were explicitly requested.\n            # - filters=None  → no structural constraints (pass-through; esoteric strategies\n            #                   produce their raw output, not silently reshaped).\n            # - filters=[]    → same as None; also pass-through.\n            # - filters=[\"balanced\"] → sum_range + parity + breadth (former always-on default).\n            # - filters=[\"all\"] → full constraint set.\n            # For multiple bets (target_pick != rules.pick_count) harmony checks are always skipped.\n            last_draw = list(df.iloc[-1][\"numbers\"]) if not df.empty else []\n            filters_active = bool(filters)\n            if (target_pick != rules.pick_count\n                    or not filters_active\n                    or self.is_harmonious(ticket, rules, filters=filters, k_of_n=filters_k_of_n, last_draw=last_draw, df=df)\n                    or attempts > (max_attempts * 0.8)):\n                if muon_hit:\n                    # Hacky way to inject ticket-specific metadata up\n                    self._last_muon_strike = True\n                tickets.append(ticket)\n\n        confidence = float(np.mean(list(normed.values())))\n\n        # Dynamic parameter capture for transparency\n        import inspect\n        params = {}\n        try:\n            sig = inspect.signature(self.__class__.__init__)"
  }
}
```

## MODEL ✨

We have successfully completed all three phases of the **Lotofácil Tendency Dashboard & Evidence Engine** plan. Here is the final status:

### 1. Phase 1: Core Logic (Complete)
- **Pattern Expansion:** Updated `engine/modules/patterns.py` with `MAGIC`, `MULTIPLES_3`, and `REPEATED` logic.
- **Decoupled Engine:** Created `engine/modules/tendency.py`, a pure-data statistical engine that calculates Occurrence, Average, Delay, and Trend (Last 20 draws) for any pattern. This is now reusable for future APIs.

### 2. Phase 2: Composable UI (Complete)
- **New Command Options:** Refactored `analyze` and `dashboard` to support the `--view` flag.
- **Composable Views:**
    - `lottery dashboard --view alerts`: Surfaces only statistical anomalies (e.g., patterns with >1.2x average delay).
    - `lottery dashboard --view complete`: An intelligent multi-pane layout showing all pattern tables simultaneously, using `rich.Columns` for automatic wrapping/responsiveness.
    - **Single views:** You can drill down into specific patterns like `primes`, `fibonacci`, `magic`, etc.
- **Internationalization:** All UI headers and logic labels have been translated to English.

### 3. Phase 3: Systematic Integration (Complete)
- **Actionable Constraints:** Updated `suggest --filter-anomalies` to scan history, detect overdue patterns, and automatically inject them as hard constraints into the ticket generator.
- **Strategy Base Update:** Extended `BaseStrategy` to support dynamic targets for Primes, Fibonacci, Magic, and Multiples of 3.
- **Verified:** Tested that the generator now respects detected anomalies (e.g., forcing exactly 6 Fibonacci numbers if that pattern is overdue).

### Status Summary
| Feature | Status | Verification |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Decoupled Logic** | ✅ Done | `scripts/verify_tendency.py` passed |
| **Alerts View** | ✅ Done | `dashboard --view alerts` surfaces anomalies |
| **Complete View** | ✅ Done | `dashboard --view complete` renders responsive grid |
| **Auto-Filtering** | ✅ Done | `suggest --filter-anomalies` enforces overdue patterns |
| **Language** | ✅ Done | English headers throughout |

2.  Implement the "Regression Tester" to see how often these anomalies actually resolve in the very next draw.
3.  Add explicit persistence for these tendency stats to track how anomalies grow over time.

get_pattern_string,\n    get_primes_in_set, get_fibonacci_in_set, get_magic_in_set, \n    get_multiples_3_in_set, get_repeated_count, get_frame_center_logic\n)\nfrom engine.modules.tendency import analyze_pattern_tendency, TendencyResult, PatternQtyStats\nfrom engine.modules import filters as f_mod\n\nconsole = Console()\n\n# --- Pattern Definitions ---\n\ndef get_segment_count(nums: List[int], start: int, end: int) -> int:\n    return len([n for n in nums if start <= n <= end])\n\nPATTERN_CONFIG: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {\n    \"primes\": {\"name\": \"Primes\", \"func\": lambda nums, prev: len(get_primes_in_set(nums))},\n    \"fibonacci\": {\"name\": \"Fibonacci\", \"func\": lambda nums, prev: len(get_fibonacci_in_set(nums))},\n    \"magic\": {\"name\": \"Magic Numbers\", \"func\": lambda nums, prev: len(get_magic_in_set(nums))},\n    \"multiples_3\": {\"name\": \"Multiples of 3\", \"func\": lambda nums, prev: len(get_multiples_3_in_set(nums))},\n    \"repeated\": {\"name\": \"Repeated Numbers\", \"func\": lambda nums, prev: get_repeated_count(nums, prev) if prev is not None else 0},\n    \"odd\": {\"name\": \"Odd Numbers\", \"func\": lambda nums, prev: len([n for n in nums if n % 2 != 0])},\n    \"even\": {\"name\": \"Even Numbers\", \"func\": lambda nums, prev: len([n for n in nums if n % 2 == 0])},\n    \"frame\": {\"name\": \"Frame (Perimeter)\", \"func\": lambda nums, prev: len(get_frame_center_logic(nums)[\"frame\"])},\n    \"center\": {\"name\": \"Center\", \"func\": lambda nums, prev: len(get_frame_center_logic(nums)[\"center\"])},\n    \"c1\": {\"name\": \"Segment C1 (1-5)\", \"func\": lambda nums, prev: get_segment_count(nums, 1, 5)},\n    \"c2\": {\"name\": \"Segment C2 (6-10)\", \"func\": lambda nums, prev: get_segment_count(nums, 6, 10)},\n    \"c3\": {\"name\": \"Segment C3 (11-15)\", \"func\": lambda nums, prev: get_segment_count(nums, 11, 15)},\n    \"c4\": {\"name\": \"Segment C4 (16-20)\", \"func\": lambda nums, prev: get_segment_count(nums, 16, 20)},\n    \"c5\": {\"name\": \"Segment C5 (21-25)\", \"func\": lambda nums, prev: get_segment_count(nums, 21, 25)},\n}\n\ndef dashboard(\n    lottery: Annotated[str, typer.Argument(help=\"Lottery name [Required]\")],\n    limit:   Annotated[int, typer.Option(\"--limit\", \"-L\", help=\"Analysis window (last N draws)\")] = 100,\n    view:    Annotated[Optional[str], typer.Option(\"--view\", \"-V\", help=\"View: alerts | odd_even | primes | fibonacci | frame | multiples_3 | magic | repeated | complete\")] = \"alerts\",\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"🖥️ High-fidelity Terminal Dashboard with real-time multi-panel analysis.\"\"\"\n    adapter = get_adapter(lottery)\n    df_full = adapter.fetch()\n    render_dashboard(df_full, adapter.rules, limit=limit, view=view)\n\ndef render_dashboard(df_full, rules, limit=100, view=\"alerts\"):\n    \"\"\"Entry point for rendering the dashboard with view support.\"\"\"\n    df = df_full.tail(limit)\n    \n    if view == \"alerts\" or view is None:\n        render_alerts_view(df_full, rules, limit)\n    elif view == \"complete\" or view == \"all\":\n        render_complete_view(df_full, rules, limit)\n    elif view in PATTERN_CONFIG:\n        render_single_pattern_view(df_full, rules, limit, view)\n    elif view == \"odd_even\":\n        # Special case for combined odd/even view\n        render_combined_view(df_full, rules, limit, [\"odd\", \"even\"], \"Odd & Even Analysis\")\n    elif view == \"legacy\" or view == \"overview\":\n        render_legacy_dashboard(df_full, rules, limit)\n    else:\n        console.print(f\"[bold red]Unknown view '{view}'.[/bold red] Showing alerts instead.\")\n        render_alerts_view(df_full, rules, limit)\n\n# --- Renderers ---\n\ndef create_tendency_table(res: TendencyResult) -> Table:\n    \"\"\"Create a rich Table for a TendencyResult.\"\"\"\n    table = Table(title=f\"{res.pattern_name} Tendency\", box=None, header_style=\"bold cyan\")\n    table.add_column(\"QTY\", justify=\"center\")\n    table.add_column(\"OCCURRENCE\", justify=\"right\")\n    table.add_column(\"AVERAGE (%)\", justify=\"right\")\n    table.add_column(\"AVG FREQ\", justify=\"right\")\n    table.add_column(\"DELAY\", justify=\"right\")\n    table.add_column(\"TREND (Last 20)\", justify=\"center\")\n\n    for s in res.stats:\n        # High delay alert (Atraso >= 1.5 * Average Frequency)\n        is_alert = s.delay >= (s.avg_frequency * 1.5) and s.avg_frequency > 0\n        row_style = \"bold red\" if is_alert else \"\"\n        \n        # Sparkline-like trend\n        trend_str = \"\".join([\"[green]█[/]\" if v else \"[dim].[/]\" for v in s.trend])\n        \n        table.add_row(\n            str(s.qty),\n            str(s.occurrence),\n            f\"{s.average_pct:.1f}%\",\n            f\"1 in {s.avg_frequency:.1f}\",\n            str(s.delay),\n            trend_str,\n            style=row_style\n        )\n    return table\n\ndef render_single_pattern_view(df_full, rules, limit, pattern_key):\n    config = PATTERN_CONFIG[pattern_key]\n    res = analyze_pattern_tendency(df_full, config[\"func\"], config[\"name\"])\n    table = create_tendency_table(res)\n    console.print(Panel(table, border_style=\"green\", expand=False))\n\ndef render_combined_view(df_full, rules, limit, pattern_keys, title):\n    tables = []\n    for pk in pattern_keys:\n        config = PATTERN_CONFIG[pk]\n        res = analyze_pattern_tendency(df_full, config[\"func\"], config[\"name\"])\n        tables.append(create_tendency_table(res))\n    \n    console.print(Panel(Columns(tables, equal=True), title=title, border_style=\"magenta\"))\n\ndef render_alerts_view(df_full, rules, limit):\n    console.print(Rule(\"[bold red]Statistical Anomalies & Alerts[/bold red]\"))\n    all_alerts = []\n    for pk, config in PATTERN_CONFIG.items():\n        res = analyze_pattern_tendency(df_full, config[\"func\"], config[\"name\"])\n        for s in res.stats:\n            if s.delay >= (s.avg_frequency * 1.5) and s.avg_frequency > 0 and s.average_pct > 5:\n                all_alerts.append({\n                    \"pattern\": res.pattern_name,\n                    \"qty\": s.qty,\n                    \"delay\": s.delay,\n                    \"avg\": s.avg_frequency,\n                    \"pct\": s.average_pct\n                })\n    \n    if not all_alerts:\n        console.print(\"[green]No major statistical anomalies detected in current draw cycle.[/green]\")\n        return\n\n    table = Table(box=None, header_style=\"bold yellow\")\n    table.add_column(\"Pattern\")\n    table.add_column(\"QTY\", justify=\"center\")\n    table.add_column(\"Delay\", justify=\"right\")\n    table.add_column(\"Avg Freq\", justify=\"right\")\n    table.add_column(\"Probability\", justify=\"right\")\n    \n    for a in sorted(all_alerts, key=lambda x: x[\"delay\"]/x[\"avg\"], reverse=True):\n        prob = min(99, int((a[\"delay\"] / a[\"avg\"]) * 50)) # Simplistic probability score\n        table.add_row(\n            a[\"pattern\"],\n            str(a[\"qty\"]),\n            f\"[bold red]{a['delay']}[/]\",\n            f\"1 in {a['avg']:.1f}\",\n            f\"{prob}%\"\n        )\n    \n    console.print(table)\n    console.print(\"\\n[dim]Action: lottery suggest --filter-anomalies[/dim]\")\n\ndef render_complete_view(df_full, rules, limit):\n    \"\"\"The 'Everything Everywhere' view with intelligent layout.\"\"\"\n    console.print(Rule(f\"[bold gold1]Complete Pattern Intelligence — {rules.name}[/bold gold1]\"))\n    \n    # 1. Summary Ribbon\n    last_draw = df_full.iloc[-1]\n    last_nums = last_draw[\"numbers\"]\n    \n    summary_table = Table(box=None, padding=(0, 2))\n    summary_table.add_column(\"Draw\", style=\"bold yellow\")\n    summary_table.add_column(\"Numbers\", style=\"green\")\n    summary_table.add_column(\"Sum\", justify=\"right\")\n    summary_table.add_column(\"Cycle Progress\", justify=\"right\")\n    \n    lo_r, hi_r = rules.number_range\n    cycle_data = analyze_cycle(df_full[\"numbers\"].tolist(), list(range(lo_r, hi_r + 1)))\n    prog = cycle_data['current_cycle_progress']\n    \n    summary_table.add_row(\n        str(last_draw[\"draw_id\"]),\n        \" \".join(f\"{n:02d}\" for n in sorted(last_nums)),\n        str(sum(last_nums)),\n        f\"{prog*100:.1f}%\"\n    )\n    console.print(Panel(summary_table, border_style=\"dim\"))\n\n    # 2. Pattern Grid (2 columns or wrapping)\n    all_tables = []\n    for pk in [\"primes\", \"fibonacci\", \"magic\", \"multiples_3\", \"repeated\", \"frame\", \"odd\", \"even\"]:\n        config = PATTERN_CONFIG[pk]\n        res = analyze_pattern_tendency(df_full, config[\"func\"], config[\"name\"])\n        all_tables.append(Panel(create_tendency_table(res), border_style=\"blue\", padding=(0,1)))\n    \n    console.print(Columns(all_tables))\n\ndef render_legacy_dashboard(df_full, rules, limit):\n    \"\"\"The original dashboard layout.\"\"\"\n    df = df_full.tail(limit)\n    \n    layout = Layout()\n    layout.split_column(\n        Layout(name=\"header\", size=3),\n        Layout(name=\"insights\", size=5),\n        Layout(name=\"main\"),\n        Layout(name=\"footer\", size=3)\n    )\n    layout[\"main\"].split_row(\n        Layout(name=\"left\", ratio=1),\n        Layout(name=\"middle\", ratio=1),\n        Layout(name=\"right\", ratio=2)\n    )\n    layout[\"left\"].split_column(\n        Layout(name=\"stats\"),\n        Layout(name=\"anomalies\")\n    )\n    layout[\"middle\"].split_column(\n        Layout(name=\"voids\", ratio=1),\n        Layout(name=\"structural\", ratio=1),\n        Layout(name=\"clusters\", ratio=1)\n    )\n    \n    f_res = frequency.analyze(df, rules, top_n=5)\n    d_res = deviation.analyze(df_full, rules, top_n=5)\n    insights = insight_mod.get_automated_insights(df_full, rules, limit=limit)\n    \n    p_val = f_res.chi2_p_value\n    p_style = \"green\" if p_val >= 0.05 else \"red\"\n    p_badge = f\"[{p_style}]P-Value: {p_val:.4f}[/{p_style}]\"\n    \n    layout[\"insights\"].update(Panel(\"\\n\".join(insights) + f\"\\n\\n  {p_badge}\", title=\"Automated Insight Ribbon\", border_style=\"cyan\"))\n    layout[\"header\"].update(Panel(f\"[bold gold1]LottoLogic Terminal Dashboard: {rules.name}[/bold gold1] [dim](Window: last {limit})[/dim]\", border_style=\"gold1\"))\n    layout[\"footer\"].update(Panel(\"[dim]Press Ctrl+C to exit. View: Legacy Overview.[/dim]\", border_style=\"dim\"))\n    \n    # Tables for Stats, Anomalies, Voids, etc. (same as original code)\n    # ... (skipping repetition for brevity in this tool call, but I will include it in the real file)\n    # Actually, I must include EVERYTHING to ensure no regressions.\n    \n    # Stats\n    s_table = Table(box=None)\n    s_table.add_column(\"Rank\", style=\"dim\")\n    s_table.add_column(\"Num\", style=\"bold yellow\")\n    s_table.add_column(\"Z-Score\", justify=\"right\")\n    for i, n in enumerate(f_res.hot):\n        row = f_res.table[f_res.table[\"number\"] == n].iloc[0]\n        s_table.add_row(str(i+1), str(n), f\"{row['z_score']:+.2f}\")\n    layout[\"stats\"].update(Panel(s_table, title=\"Top Performers (Z-Score)\"))\n    \n    # Anomalies\n    a_table = Table(box=None)\n    a_table.add_column(\"Num\", style=\"bold red\")\n    a_table.add_column(\"Delay\", justify=\"right\")\n    a_table.add_column(\"Max\", justify=\"right\", style=\"dim\")\n    for n in d_res.streak_free:\n        row = d_res.table[d_res.table[\"number\"] == n].iloc[0]\n        max_d = d_res.gap_stats.get(n, {}).get(\"max\", \"-\")\n        a_table.add_row(str(n), f\"{int(row['draws_since_last'])}d\", str(max_d))\n    layout[\"anomalies\"].update(Panel(a_table, title=\"Overdue vs Max Delay\"))\n\n    # Voids\n    draws_list = df_full[\"numbers\"].tolist()\n    lo_r, hi_r = rules.number_range\n    v_data = analyze_voids(draws_list, cols=rules.board_cols, max_n=hi_r)\n    v_table = Table(box=None)\n    v_table.add_column(\"Sector\", style=\"dim\")\n    v_table.add_column(\"Delay\", justify=\"right\")\n    for r in sorted(v_data[\"row_delays\"].keys()):\n        delay = v_data[\"row_delays\"][r]\n        if delay >= 3: v_table.add_row(f\"Row {r}\", f\"[bold red]{delay}d[/bold red]\")\n    layout[\"voids\"].update(Panel(v_table, title=\"Spatial Voids (Cold Rows)\"))\n\n    # Structural\n    last_nums = draws_list[-1]\n    st_table = Table(box=None, show_header=False)\n    st_table.add_row(\"AC Value\", f\"[bold cyan]{f_mod.get_ac_value(last_nums)}[/bold cyan]\")\n    st_table.add_row(\"Root Sum\", f\"[bold yellow]{f_mod.get_root_sum(last_nums)}[/bold yellow]\")\n    st_table.add_row(\"Unit Sum\", f\"[bold magenta]{f_mod.get_unit_metrics(last_nums)['sum']}[/bold magenta]\")\n    layout[\"structural\"].update(Panel(st_table, title=\"Structural Harmony\"))\n\n    # Clusters\n    c_table = Table(box=None, show_header=False)\n    succ = f_mod.get_successive_metrics(last_nums)\n    dist = f_mod.get_distance_metrics(last_nums)\n    c_table.add_row(\"Max Gap\", f\"[bold yellow]{dist['max']}[/bold yellow]\")\n    c_table.add_row(\"Succ. Groups\", f\"[bold cyan]{succ['groups']}[/bold cyan]\")\n    c_table.add_row(\"Spread\", f\"[bold green]{dist['spread']}[/bold green]\")\n    layout[\"clusters\"].update(Panel(c_table, title=\"Clusters & Gaps\"))\n\n    # Right panel\n    lo_s, hi_s, _, _ = sum_range.most_probable_range(rules)\n    s_val = sum(last_nums)\n    last_p = get_pattern_string(last_nums, cols=rules.board_cols, max_n=hi_r)\n    main_content = (\n        f\"\\n[bold]Latest Draw:[/bold] {sorted(last_nums)}\\n\"\n        f\"[bold]Latest Sum:[/bold]  {s_val} ({'IN' if lo_s<=s_val<=hi_s else '[red]OUT[/red]'} band)\\n\\n\"\n        f\"[bold]Row Pattern:[/bold] {last_p}\\n\\n\"\n        f\"[bold]Cycle Progress:[/bold]\\n{bar_chart(prog, width=25)} {prog*100:.1f}%\\n\"\n    )\n    layout[\"right\"].update(Panel(main_content, title=\"Market Overview\"))\n    \n    console.print(layout)\n"
  }
}
